Here is the refactored, modular project structure. The monolithic file has been split into **CSS modules**, **Data modules**, **Utility modules**, **Core Engine modules**, and **View components** using native ES Modules.

### Project Directory Structure

```text
study-plan/
├── index.html                        # App shell, navigation & Session Detail HTML template
├── css/
│   ├── 01-base-and-shell.css         # Variables, resets, header, bottom nav, desktop sidebar, buttons
│   ├── 02-plan-and-day-cards.css     # Plan calendar, Day study card & notebook paper artwork
│   ├── 03-editors-wizard-settings.css# Setup wizard, form sliders, unit checklists & settings
│   ├── 04-schedule-and-modal.css     # 24-hr interactive calendar grid & New/Edit Session sheet
│   └── 05-session-detail.css         # Scoped styles (.sess-scope) for the Session Detail screen
└── js/
    ├── main.js                       # Entry point, tab router, keyboard shortcuts & global bindings
    ├── state.js                      # App state, localStorage persistence & profile management
    ├── data/
    │   ├── icons.js                  # UI action icons & subject SVG illustrations
    │   ├── subjects.js               # Departments, subjects, units & syllabus hour weights
    │   ├── systems.js                # Hosted interactive study system URLs & unit anchors
    │   ├── resources.js              # Textbook PDF cutouts & syllabus links per subject/module
    │   └── pyq-optics.js             # Previous Year Questions (PYQ) dataset for Optics
    ├── utils/
    │   ├── dom.js                    # DOM selectors ($), HTML escaping, toast & empty states
    │   ├── dates.js                  # Date/time parsing, formatting & calendar grid math
    │   └── export-image.js           # PNG export helper using modern-screenshot
    ├── engine/
    │   ├── syllabus.js               # Syllabus queries, remaining units & session time costs
    │   └── planner.js                # Study plan scheduling & revision allocation algorithm
    └── views/
        ├── shared-editors.js         # Reusable Profile, Rules, Schedule & Progress editors
        ├── wizard.js                 # 4-step initial Setup Wizard controller
        ├── plan-view.js              # Plan tab (weekly roadmap grid)
        ├── day-view.js               # Day tab (daily study card preview & actions)
        ├── schedule-view.js          # Schedule tab (24-hr drag/resize/swipe calendar grid)
        ├── session-modal.js          # Bottom sheet modal for creating/editing custom sessions
        ├── session-detail-view.js    # Session Detail view (Units, Resources, PYQ & Start modal)
        └── settings-view.js          # Settings tab, JSON import/export & reset controls
```

--- START OF FILE js/utils/dom.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   DOM QUERY, HTML ESCAPING, TOAST & EMPTY STATE HELPERS
   ========================================================================= */

export const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);

export const esc = (s) =>
  String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
    "&": "&amp;",
    "<": "&lt;",
    ">": "&gt;",
    '"': "&quot;",
    "'": "&#39;"
  }[c]));

export const clone = (o) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));

export function toast(msg) {
  const t = $("#toast");
  if (!t) return;
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.classList.add("show");
  clearTimeout(toast._t);
  toast._t = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove("show"), 2400);
}

export function emptyHtml(t, p) {
  return `<div class="card empty empty-hero">
    <span class="eh-pill">Nothing here yet</span>
    <h2>${t || "Let's build your plan"}</h2>
    <p class="eh-sub">${p || "Answer a few quick questions and your study plan will appear here."}</p>
    <button class="btn gold eh-cta" onclick="openWizard(false)">Start setup</button>
  </div>`;
}
--- END OF FILE js/utils/dom.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/utils/dates.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   DATE, TIME & CALENDAR GRID MATH HELPERS
   ========================================================================= */

// "?now=2026-10-22T10:00" overrides the clock for testing
const NOW_OVERRIDE = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("now");

export const now = () => NOW_OVERRIDE ? new Date(NOW_OVERRIDE) : new Date();
export const iso = (d) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
export const parse = (s) => new Date(s + "T00:00:00");
export const addDays = (s, n) => { const d = parse(s); d.setDate(d.getDate() + n); return iso(d); };
export const daysBetween = (a, b) => Math.round((parse(b) - parse(a)) / 864e5);
export const todayISO = () => iso(now());

// Before 19:00 the "current" day is today; from 19:00 on it is tomorrow.
export const focusISO = () => now().getHours() >= 19 ? addDays(todayISO(), 1) : todayISO();

export const WD = ["Sunday", "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"];
export const MON = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];

export const wdName = (s) => WD[parse(s).getDay()];
export const wdShort = (s) => wdName(s).slice(0, 3);
export const monName = (s) => MON[parse(s).getMonth()];
export const dayNum = (s) => Number(s.slice(8));
export const niceDate = (s) => `${dayNum(s)} ${monName(s)}`;

export const rangeDates = (a, b) => {
  const out = [];
  for (let d = a; d <= b; d = addDays(d, 1)) out.push(d);
  return out;
};

export const fmtH = (h) => `${Math.round(h * 10) / 10}`.replace(/\.0$/, "");
export const hrs = (h) => `${fmtH(h)} ${h === 1 ? "hr" : "hrs"}`;
export const hm = (h) => `${Math.round(h * 60)} min`;

// Schedule Grid Constants & Time Math
export const SCH_PX_H = 120;          // pixels per hour
export const SCH_PX_MIN = SCH_PX_H / 60;
export const SCH_GUT = 62;            // gutter for hour labels
export const SCH_SNAP = 15;           // minutes
export const SCH_EDGE = 10;           // px from a block edge that counts as a resize handle

export const toMin = (t) => { const [h, m] = String(t).split(":").map(Number); return h * 60 + m; };
export const hhmm = (m) => `${String(Math.floor(m / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
export const snapMin = (m) => Math.round(m / SCH_SNAP) * SCH_SNAP;
export const clampMin = (m) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1440, m));
export const durMin = (x) => toMin(x.end) - toMin(x.start);
--- END OF FILE js/utils/dates.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/utils/export-image.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   PNG IMAGE EXPORT HELPER (modern-screenshot)
   ========================================================================= */

import { toast } from "./dom.js";

// Let the preview reflow naturally; downloads use the full editorial layout.
export async function exportPng(el, filename, fixedWidth) {
  if (!el || exportPng.busy) return;
  if (!window.modernScreenshot) {
    toast("Image export needs an internet connection (library didn't load)");
    return;
  }
  exportPng.busy = true;
  const copy = el.cloneNode(true);
  copy.classList.add("exporting");
  copy.style.width = (fixedWidth || Number(el.dataset.exportWidth) || el.offsetWidth) + "px";
  copy.style.maxWidth = "none";
  copy.style.position = "absolute";
  copy.style.left = "-20000px";
  copy.style.top = "0";
  copy.style.transform = "none";
  copy.style.boxShadow = "none";
  copy.querySelectorAll(".no-export").forEach(n => n.remove());
  copy.querySelectorAll(".done,.past,.today").forEach(n => n.classList.remove("done", "past", "today"));
  document.body.appendChild(copy);
  try {
    await document.fonts.ready;
    const url = await window.modernScreenshot.domToPng(copy, {
      scale: el.id === "day-card" ? 1080 / copy.offsetWidth : 2,
      width: copy.offsetWidth,
      height: copy.offsetHeight,
      backgroundColor: "#ffffff"
    });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.download = filename;
    a.href = url;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    toast("Saved " + filename);
  } catch (err) {
    toast("Export failed: " + err.message);
  } finally {
    copy.remove();
    exportPng.busy = false;
  }
}
--- END OF FILE js/utils/export-image.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/engine/syllabus.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   SYLLABUS QUERIES, UNIT COST & PROGRESS CALCULATIONS
   ========================================================================= */

import { DEPTS, SUBJECTS, SYLLABUS_HOURS_MAP, UNIT_MODULE, MOD_ROMAN } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { toMin } from "../utils/dates.js";

const UNIT_REF_RE = /\s*\((?:Units?\s*)?[\d.]+(?:\s*[–-]\s*[\d.]+)?\)\s*$/;
export const cleanUnit = (t) => t.replace(UNIT_REF_RE, "");

export const TIME_SCALE = 1.25; // unit times are divided by this for every subject
export const unitHrs = (id, u) => ((SYLLABUS_HOURS_MAP[id] || [])[u - 1] ?? 2) / TIME_SCALE;
export const unitMod = (id, u) => (UNIT_MODULE[id] || "")[u - 1] || "4";

export function subj(id, cfg) {
  const s = SUBJECTS[id];
  if (id === "SEC_OTH" && cfg && cfg.secName.trim()) {
    return { ...s, name: cfg.secName.trim(), shortName: cfg.secName.trim() };
  }
  return s;
}

export const activeIds = (cfg) => [...DEPTS[cfg.dept].ids, cfg.sec];
export const scheduleIds = (cfg) => activeIds(cfg);

export const examsOf = (cfg) =>
  activeIds(cfg)
    .map(id => subj(id, cfg))
    .sort((a, b) => a.examDate.localeCompare(b.examDate) || a.role.localeCompare(b.role));

export const doneSet = (cfg, id) => new Set(cfg.done[id] || []);

export const remainingUnits = (cfg, id) => {
  const d = doneSet(cfg, id);
  return SUBJECTS[id].units.filter(u => !d.has(u.u)).map(u => ({ ...u, h: unitHrs(id, u.u) }));
};

export const colorStyle = (id) => {
  const t = SUBJECTS[id].theme;
  return `--bg2:${t.bannerBg};--bd:${t.border}`;
};

// Session Maker & Stage Depth Constants
export const MODE_NAMES = ["Revision", "Read", "Deep Dive", "Review"];
export const MODE_MULT = [0.5, 1, 2, 0.25]; // Revision = 0.5x, Read = 1x, Deep Dive = 2x, Review = 0.25x
export const MODE_SLIDER = [3, 0, 1, 2];    // Left-to-right order: Review, Revision, Read, Deep Dive

export const modulesOf = (id) => [...new Set((UNIT_MODULE[id] || "").split(""))].sort();
export const modUnits = (id, m) => SUBJECTS[id].units.filter(u => unitMod(id, u.u) === m);

export const coursePct = (cfg, id, m) => {
  const list = m ? modUnits(id, m) : SUBJECTS[id].units;
  const dset = doneSet(cfg, id);
  return list.length ? Math.round(100 * list.filter(u => dset.has(u.u)).length / list.length) : 0;
};

export const normUnits = (arr) => (arr || []).map(v => typeof v === "number" ? { u: v, mode: 1 } : v);

export const usedMods = (draft) => {
  const m = [...new Set(draft.units.map(v => unitMod(draft.subjectId, v.u)))].sort();
  return m.length ? m : [draft.moduleNo];
};

export function sessionName(cfg, subjectId, m) {
  const list = [].concat(m).slice().sort();
  const romans = list.map(x => (MOD_ROMAN[x] || "Module " + x).replace("Module ", "")).join(", ");
  return `${subj(subjectId, cfg).name} · ${list.length > 1 ? "Modules" : "Module"} ${romans}`;
}

export const unitCost = (id, u, mode) => unitHrs(id, u) * MODE_MULT[mode];
export const usedHrs = (draft) => draft.units.reduce((t, v) => t + unitCost(draft.subjectId, v.u, v.mode), 0);
export const availHrs = (draft) => Math.max(0, (toMin(draft.end) - toMin(draft.start)) / 60);
export const fitsHrs = (used, avail) => used <= avail + 1e-9;
--- END OF FILE js/engine/syllabus.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/engine/planner.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   STUDY PLAN GENERATION ENGINE
   ========================================================================= */

import { SUBJECTS, LAST_EXAM } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { rangeDates, addDays } from "../utils/dates.js";
import { activeIds, remainingUnits, subj } from "./syllabus.js";

export const planDates = (c) => rangeDates(c.start, addDays(LAST_EXAM, -1));
export const slotCount = (c, d) => Math.max(0, Math.min(c.maxPerDay, c.days[d] ?? c.maxPerDay));
export const totalSlots = (c, from) => planDates({ ...c, start: from || c.start }).reduce((n, d) => n + slotCount(c, d), 0);

// Sessions each subject needs at the chosen session length (SEC uses the number the user asked for).
export function sessionNeeds(c) {
  const out = {};
  activeIds(c).forEach(id => {
    const rem = remainingUnits(c, id);
    const h = rem.reduce((s, u) => s + u.h, 0);
    out[id] = {
      units: rem.length,
      hours: h,
      need: rem.length ? Math.min(rem.length, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(h / c.hours - 1e-9))) : 0
    };
  });
  if (out[c.sec].units) out[c.sec].need = Math.min(c.secSessions, out[c.sec].units);
  return out;
}

export function revisionBullets(id) {
  const b = (SUBJECTS[id].revisionBullets || []).map(t =>
    t.replace(/^Full revision:\s*/i, "").replace(/\s*\([^()]*\)\s*$/, "")
  );
  return b.length ? b : ["Revise every unit end to end", "Redo the toughest problems"];
}

export function buildPlan(c) {
  const ids = activeIds(c);
  const dates = planDates(c);
  const slots = [];
  dates.forEach(d => {
    for (let i = 0; i < slotCount(c, d); i++) slots.push({ date: d, slot: i });
  });
  const T = slots.length;
  const needs = sessionNeeds(c);

  // A subject's teaching sessions never exceed the session length, so its hours are spread over
  // ceil(hours / length) sessions. If the slots cannot hold all of that, the shortfall is reported.
  ids.filter(id => id !== c.sec && needs[id].need).forEach(id => {
    needs[id].sessionHours = needs[id].hours / needs[id].need;
  });

  const demand = ids.reduce((s, id) => s + needs[id].need + (needs[id].units ? c.revision : 0), 0);
  const shortfall = demand > T
    ? `Not enough slots: ${demand} sessions needed, ${T} available at ${c.maxPerDay} per day. Extra sessions go over the daily limit.`
    : null;

  const deadline = id => SUBJECTS[id].examDate;
  // SEC and the Elective 2 subjects (Materials Science, Machine Learning I) are placed only on 30 Oct to 1 Nov.
  const inWindow = (id, date) =>
    (id === c.sec || id === "MS" || id === "ML")
      ? (date >= "2026-10-30" && date <= "2026-11-01" && date < deadline(id))
      : date < deadline(id);

  const order = ids.slice().sort((a, b) => SUBJECTS[a].examDate.localeCompare(SUBJECTS[b].examDate));
  const placed = Object.fromEntries(ids.map(id => [id, []]));
  const assignments = Array(T).fill(null);
  const firstTeach = {}, firstRev = {};
  const assign = (i, id, type) => { assignments[i] = { id, type }; };

  // Keep at least one teaching session for every unfinished subject, then reserve
  // revision nearest its exam. Revision must not disappear when teaching is heavy.
  order.forEach(id => {
    if (!needs[id].need) return;
    const i = slots.findIndex((slot, j) => !assignments[j] && inWindow(id, slot.date));
    if (i >= 0) { assign(i, id, "teach"); firstTeach[id] = i; }
  });

  for (let round = 0; round < c.revision; round++) {
    order.forEach(id => {
      if (needs[id].need && firstTeach[id] === undefined) return;
      for (let i = T - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const sameDayRev = assignments.some((a, j) => a && a.id === id && a.type === "rev" && slots[j].date === slots[i].date);
        if (!assignments[i] && !sameDayRev && inWindow(id, slots[i].date) && i > (firstTeach[id] ?? -1)) {
          assign(i, id, "rev"); firstRev[id] = i; break;
        }
      }
    });
  }

  const teachCounts = Object.fromEntries(ids.map(id => [id, firstTeach[id] === undefined ? 0 : 1]));
  let curDate = null, usedToday = new Set();

  slots.forEach((slot, i) => {
    if (slot.date !== curDate) { curDate = slot.date; usedToday = new Set(); }
    if (!assignments[i]) {
      let best = null, bestScore = -1;
      order.forEach(id => {
        if (teachCounts[id] >= needs[id].need || !inWindow(id, slot.date) || i >= (firstRev[id] ?? T)) return;
        const left = slots.reduce((n, next, j) => n + (j >= i && !assignments[j] && inWindow(id, next.date) && j < (firstRev[id] ?? T) ? 1 : 0), 0);
        let score = (needs[id].need - teachCounts[id]) / Math.max(1, left);
        if (usedToday.has(id)) score *= 0.7;
        if (score > bestScore) { best = id; bestScore = score; }
      });
      if (best) { assign(i, best, "teach"); teachCounts[best]++; }
    }
    if (assignments[i]) {
      const { id, type } = assignments[i];
      placed[id].push({ date: slot.date, slot: slot.slot, type });
      usedToday.add(id);
    }
  });

  // Session counts and hours are preferences, not limits on syllabus coverage.
  // Add any missing teaching/revision before each upcoming exam.
  const extraSlot = date => Math.max(-1, ...Object.values(placed).flat().filter(p => p.date === date).map(p => p.slot)) + 1;
  const dayLoad = date => Object.values(placed).flat().filter(p => p.date === date).length;
  const freeDay = (days, id, type) =>
    days.find(date => dayLoad(date) < slotCount(c, date) && !(type && placed[id].some(p => p.date === date && p.type === type)));

  ids.forEach(id => {
    const available = dates.filter(date => inWindow(id, date));
    if (!available.length) return;
    const selected = available.filter(date => slotCount(c, date) > 0);
    const studyDays = selected.length ? selected : [available[available.length - 1]];
    const mine = placed[id];

    if (needs[id].need && !mine.some(p => p.type === "teach")) {
      const order = [...studyDays, ...available];
      const date = freeDay(order, id, "teach") ?? freeDay(order, id, null) ?? studyDays[0];
      mine.push({ date, slot: extraSlot(date), type: "teach" });
    }
    const missing = Math.max(0, c.revision - mine.filter(p => p.type === "rev").length);
    for (let pass = 0; pass < missing; pass++) {
      const order = [...studyDays, ...available].reverse();
      const date = freeDay(order, id, "rev") ?? freeDay(order, id, null) ?? studyDays[studyDays.length - 1];
      mine.push({ date, slot: extraSlot(date), type: "rev" });
    }
    mine.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || a.slot - b.slot);
  });

  // Hand units to teaching sessions in order
  const sessions = [];
  ids.forEach(id => {
    const s = subj(id, c);
    const rem = remainingUnits(c, id);
    const total = SUBJECTS[id].units.length;
    const teach = placed[id].filter(p => p.type === "teach");
    let idx = 0, cum = 0, covered = (c.done[id] || []).length;
    const totH = rem.reduce((a, u) => a + u.h, 0);

    teach.forEach((p, i) => {
      const left = teach.length - 1 - i;
      let chunk = [];
      if (i === teach.length - 1) {
        chunk = rem.slice(idx);
        idx = rem.length;
      } else {
        const target = totH * (i + 1) / teach.length;
        while (idx < rem.length - left && (chunk.length === 0 || cum + rem[idx].h / 2 <= target)) {
          chunk.push(rem[idx]);
          cum += rem[idx].h;
          idx++;
        }
      }
      if (i === teach.length - 1) cum = totH;
      const before = covered;
      covered += chunk.length;
      p.units = chunk;
      p.before = Math.round(100 * before / total);
      p.after = Math.round(100 * covered / total);
    });

    placed[id].forEach((p, i, arr) => {
      const n = arr.slice(0, i + 1).filter(x => x.date === p.date && x.type === p.type).length;
      sessions.push({
        id: `${p.date}_${id}_${p.type}${n}`,
        date: p.date,
        slot: p.slot,
        subjectId: id,
        type: p.type,
        hours: p.type === "teach" ? needs[id].sessionHours ?? c.hours : c.hours,
        units: p.units || [],
        before: p.before ?? 100,
        after: p.after ?? 100,
        bullets: p.type === "rev" ? revisionBullets(id, s) : null
      });
    });

    // Revision numbering ("Revision 1/2")
    sessions
      .filter(x => x.subjectId === id && x.type === "rev")
      .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || a.slot - b.slot)
      .forEach((x, i, arr) => { x.revNo = arr.length > 1 ? `${i + 1}/${arr.length}` : ""; });
  });

  sessions.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || a.slot - b.slot);

  const info = {};
  ids.forEach(id => {
    const mine = sessions.filter(x => x.subjectId === id);
    info[id] = {
      total: SUBJECTS[id].units.length,
      done: (c.done[id] || []).length,
      sessions: mine.length,
      teach: mine.filter(x => x.type === "teach").length,
      rev: mine.filter(x => x.type === "rev").length,
      hours: mine.reduce((a, x) => a + x.hours, 0)
    };
  });

  return {
    sessions,
    dates: [...new Set(sessions.map(s => s.date))],
    warnings: shortfall ? [shortfall] : [],
    info,
    slots: Math.max(T, sessions.length)
  };
}

--- START OF FILE js/state.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   APPLICATION STATE & LOCALSTORAGE PERSISTENCE
   ========================================================================= */

import { focusISO } from "./utils/dates.js";
import { clone, toast } from "./utils/dom.js";
import { buildPlan } from "./engine/planner.js";

export const STORE_KEY = "study_plan_v4_fresh";
export const openUnitLists = new Set();

export function defaultCfg() {
  return {
    ready: false,
    dept: "physics",
    sec: "SEC_PHY",
    secName: "",
    hours: 1,
    maxPerDay: 3,
    secSessions: 3,
    revision: 1,
    start: focusISO(),
    days: {},
    done: {},
    custom: []
  };
}

export let cfg = defaultCfg();
export let prog = { sessions: {} };
export let profiles = {};
export let plan = null;

export const ui = {
  tab: "plan",
  viewDate: null,
  draft: null,
  step: 0,
  wizard: false,
  wizardFromSettings: false,
  dirty: false,
  settingsDraft: false,
  schedDate: null,
  schedDetail: null,
  schedEdit: false,
  schedSel: null,
  schedFocus: null,
  schedSnap: false
};

export function setCfg(nextCfg) {
  cfg = nextCfg;
}

export function setProg(nextProg) {
  prog = nextProg;
}

// v5 auto-setup: with no saved profile, start a ready Physics profile with default rules so wizard is skipped.
export function autoSetup() {
  if (Object.keys(profiles).length) return;
  profiles.physics = {
    cfg: { ...defaultCfg(), dept: "physics", ready: true },
    prog: { sessions: {} }
  };
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ active: "physics", profiles }));
  } catch (e) {
    /* storage unavailable: keep in memory */
  }
}

export function loadProfile(dept) {
  const p = profiles[dept];
  if (p && p.cfg && p.cfg.ready) {
    cfg = { ...defaultCfg(), ...clone(p.cfg) };
    prog = { sessions: {}, ...clone(p.prog || {}) };
  } else {
    cfg = { ...defaultCfg(), dept };
    prog = { sessions: {} };
  }
}

export function load() {
  try {
    const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY) || "null");
    if (raw && raw.profiles) {
      profiles = raw.profiles;
    } else if (raw && raw.cfg) {
      profiles = { [raw.cfg.dept || "physics"]: { cfg: raw.cfg, prog: raw.prog || {} } };
    }
    autoSetup();
    const active = raw && raw.active && profiles[raw.active] ? raw.active : Object.keys(profiles)[0];
    if (active) loadProfile(active);
  } catch (e) {
    /* storage unavailable: run in memory */
  }
}

export function save() {
  if (cfg.ready) {
    profiles[cfg.dept] = { cfg: clone(cfg), prog: clone(prog) };
  }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ active: cfg.dept, profiles }));
  } catch (e) {
    toast("Couldn't save to this browser");
  }
}

export function replan() {
  plan = cfg.ready
    ? buildPlan(cfg)
    : { sessions: [], dates: [], warnings: [], info: {}, slots: 0 };
}

/* =========================================================================
   SHARED EDITORS (Used by both Setup Wizard and Settings)
   ========================================================================= */

import { DEPTS, SEC_OPTIONS, LAST_EXAM, MODS, MODULE_LABEL } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { ICON, SUBJECT_SVGS } from "../data/icons.js";
import { $, esc, toast } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { focusISO, rangeDates, addDays, hrs, fmtH, niceDate, wdName, parse } from "../utils/dates.js";
import { activeIds, examsOf, remainingUnits, subj, unitMod, doneSet, unitHrs, cleanUnit } from "../engine/syllabus.js";
import { cfg, ui, save, loadProfile, replan } from "../state.js";
import { openWizard } from "./wizard.js";
import { renderAll } from "../main.js";

export function editDates(d) {
  return rangeDates(focusISO(), addDays(LAST_EXAM, -1));
}

export function normalizeDraft(d) {
  d.start = focusISO();
  d.maxPerDay = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, d.maxPerDay));
  editDates(d).forEach(x => {
    d.days[x] = Math.max(0, Math.min(d.maxPerDay, d.days[x] ?? d.maxPerDay));
  });
}

// Switching department keeps the current one's data and opens the other's (or starts its setup).
export function switchDepartment(dept) {
  if (ui.dirty && !confirm("Discard unsaved changes and switch department?")) return;
  save();
  loadProfile(dept);
  ui.settingsDraft = false;
  ui.dirty = false;
  ui.viewDate = null;
  replan();
  save();
  renderAll();
  if (!cfg.ready) openWizard(false);
  toast(`${DEPTS[dept].label} selected`);
}

export function ruleSlider(label, desc, key, d, min, max, step, fmt, onChange) {
  const row = document.createElement("div");
  row.className = "rule-row";
  row.innerHTML = `<div class="rule-copy"><label for="rule-${key}">${label}</label><p>${desc}</p></div>
    <output for="rule-${key}"></output>
    <div class="rule-control">
      <input id="rule-${key}" class="stops" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}">
      <div class="range-ends"><span>${fmt(min)}</span><span>${fmt(max)}</span></div>
    </div>`;
  const range = $("input", row), out = $("output", row);
  const paint = () => {
    range.value = d[key];
    out.textContent = fmt(d[key]);
    range.setAttribute("aria-valuetext", String(fmt(d[key])));
    range.style.setProperty("--p", ((d[key] - min) / (max - min) * 100) + "%");
  };
  range.oninput = () => {
    d[key] = Number(range.value);
    paint();
    onChange();
  };
  paint();
  return row;
}

export function renderProfile(root, d, onChange) {
  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="field-title">Department</div>
    <div class="choice-grid" id="dept-grid"></div>
    <div class="sec-block">
      <div class="field-title">SEC subject</div>
      <div class="choice-grid" id="sec-grid"></div>
      <input class="text-in" id="sec-name" placeholder="Name of your SEC (e.g. Data Science Basics)" maxlength="60" />
    </div>`;
  const dg = $("#dept-grid", root), sg = $("#sec-grid", root), nm = $("#sec-name", root);
  const paint = () => {
    dg.innerHTML = Object.entries(DEPTS)
      .map(([k, v]) => `<button type="button" class="choice ${d.dept === k ? "on" : ""}" data-k="${k}"><b>${v.label}</b><span>${v.sub}</span></button>`)
      .join("");
    sg.innerHTML = SEC_OPTIONS
      .map(o => `<button type="button" class="choice ${d.sec === o.id ? "on" : ""}" data-k="${o.id}"><b>${o.label}</b><span>${o.sub}</span></button>`)
      .join("");
    nm.style.display = d.sec === "SEC_OTH" ? "block" : "none";
    nm.value = d.secName;
    dg.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      if (d.dept !== b.dataset.k) switchDepartment(b.dataset.k);
    });
    sg.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      d.sec = b.dataset.k;
      paint();
      onChange();
    });
  };
  nm.oninput = () => { d.secName = nm.value; onChange(); };
  paint();
}

export function renderRules(root, d, onChange) {
  root.innerHTML = "";
  const re = () => {
    d.maxPerDay = Math.max(1, d.maxPerDay);
    Object.keys(d.days).forEach(k => { if (d.days[k] > d.maxPerDay) d.days[k] = d.maxPerDay; });
    onChange();
  };
  root.append(
    ruleSlider("Hours per session", "Your preferred pace; all topics are included regardless of time", "hours", d, 1, 6, 0.5, v => hrs(v), onChange),
    ruleSlider("Preferred sessions per day", "Extra sessions are added when needed to include all topics and revision", "maxPerDay", d, 1, 5, 1, v => v, re),
    ruleSlider("Sessions for SEC", "Total sessions you want to give the SEC subject", "secSessions", d, 1, 8, 1, v => v, onChange),
    ruleSlider("Revision sessions per subject", "Full-syllabus revision passes kept for the run-up to each exam", "revision", d, 0, 3, 1, v => v === 0 ? "None" : v, onChange)
  );
}

export function renderSchedule(root, d, onChange) {
  const dates = editDates(d);
  const exams = {};
  examsOf(d).forEach(s => (exams[s.examDate] = exams[s.examDate] || []).push(s.shortName));
  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="sched-tools">
      <button type="button" class="btn sm" data-a="max">Max every day</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn sm" data-a="wk">Weekends max, weekdays ${Math.max(1, d.maxPerDay - 1)}</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn sm" data-a="clr">Clear all</button>
    </div>
    <div id="sched-rows"></div>
    <div class="cap-bar" id="cap"></div>`;
  const rows = $("#sched-rows", root), cap = $("#cap", root);

  const paintCap = () => {
    const n = dates.reduce((s, x) => s + d.days[x], 0);
    cap.innerHTML = `${ICON.clock}<span><b>${n}</b> sessions &middot; <b>${fmtH(n * d.hours)}</b> hours across <b>${dates.filter(x => d.days[x] > 0).length}</b> study days</span>`;
  };

  const M = d.maxPerDay;
  const paintRows = () => {
    rows.innerHTML = "";
    if (!dates.length) {
      rows.innerHTML = `<p class="muted">No study days left before the last exam.</p>`;
      return;
    }
    dates.forEach(x => {
      const r = document.createElement("div");
      r.className = "sched-row";
      const ex = (exams[x] || []).map(n => `<span class="ex">${ICON.exam}${esc(n)} exam</span>`).join(" ");
      let ticks = "";
      for (let i = 0; i <= M; i++) {
        ticks += `<i class="mod" style="left:${i / M * 100}%"></i><em style="left:${i / M * 100}%">${i}</em>`;
      }
      r.innerHTML = `
        <div class="dl"><b>${niceDate(x)}</b><span>${wdName(x)}</span></div>
        <div class="slider-wrap">
          <input class="stops" type="range" min="0" max="${M}" step="1" aria-label="Sessions on ${niceDate(x)}">
          <div class="ticks" style="left:14px;right:14px">${ticks}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="hrs"></div>
        ${ex ? `<div class="exrow">${ex}</div>` : ""}`;
      const range = $("input", r), hr = $(".hrs", r);
      const paint = () => {
        range.value = d.days[x];
        range.style.setProperty("--p", (d.days[x] / M * 100) + "%");
        hr.innerHTML = d.days[x] ? `<b>${d.days[x]}</b> &times; ${fmtH(d.hours)}h` : `<span class="muted">Off</span>`;
      };
      range.oninput = () => {
        d.days[x] = Number(range.value);
        paint();
        paintCap();
        onChange();
      };
      r._paint = paint;
      paint();
      rows.appendChild(r);
    });
  };

  root.querySelectorAll("[data-a]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    dates.forEach(x => {
      const we = [0, 6].includes(parse(x).getDay());
      d.days[x] = b.dataset.a === "max"
        ? d.maxPerDay
        : b.dataset.a === "clr"
          ? 0
          : (we ? d.maxPerDay : Math.max(1, d.maxPerDay - 1));
    });
    rows.querySelectorAll(".sched-row").forEach(r => r._paint());
    paintCap();
    onChange();
  });

  paintRows();
  paintCap();
}

export function renderProgress(root, d, onChange) {
  root.innerHTML = `<div id="cap-need" class="cap-bar"></div><div style="height:14px"></div><div id="psubs"></div>`;
  const psubs = $("#psubs", root), capNeed = $("#cap-need", root);

  const paintNeed = () => {
    const topics = activeIds(d).reduce((n, id) => n + remainingUnits(d, id).length, 0);
    capNeed.innerHTML = `<span><b>${topics}</b> topics left · <b>${d.revision}</b> revision ${d.revision === 1 ? "pass" : "passes"} per subject. Your plan includes all remaining topics before upcoming exams.</span>`;
  };

  activeIds(d).forEach(id => {
    const s = subj(id, d);
    const N = s.units.length;
    const box = document.createElement("div");
    box.className = "psub";
    box.style.cssText = `--bg2:${s.theme.bannerBg};--bd:${s.theme.border}`;
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="psub-h">
        <div class="ic">${SUBJECT_SVGS[s.iconId]}</div>
        <div><div class="t">${esc(s.name)}</div><div class="s">${s.role} · exam ${niceDate(s.examDate)}</div></div>
        <div class="pct"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="slider-wrap">
        <input class="stops" type="range" min="0" max="${N}" step="1" aria-label="Units completed in ${esc(s.name)}">
        <div class="ticks"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="s sl-label" style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#334155;margin:0 0 8px"></div>
      <details class="unit-dd">
        <summary><span>Tick topics individually</span><span class="cnt"></span></summary>
        <div class="unit-list"></div>
      </details>`;

    const range = $("input", box),
          ticks = $(".ticks", box),
          pct = $(".pct", box),
          label = $(".sl-label", box),
          list = $(".unit-list", box),
          cnt = $(".cnt", box);

    const thumb = 14;
    let tickHtml = "";
    for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
      const left = `calc(${i / N * 100}% )`;
      const isMod = i < N && (i === 0 || unitMod(id, i + 1) !== unitMod(id, i));
      tickHtml += `<i class="${isMod || i === N ? "mod" : ""}" style="left:${left}"></i>`;
      if (isMod) tickHtml += `<em style="left:${left}">${MODULE_LABEL[unitMod(id, i + 1)]}</em>`;
    }
    ticks.innerHTML = tickHtml;
    ticks.style.left = ticks.style.right = thumb + "px";

    const paint = () => {
      const set = doneSet(d, id);
      const k = set.size;
      const prefix = [...set].every(u => u <= k);
      range.value = k;
      range.style.setProperty("--p", (k / N * 100) + "%");
      pct.textContent = Math.round(k / N * 100) + "%";
      const remH = s.units.filter(u => !set.has(u.u)).reduce((a, u) => a + unitHrs(id, u.u), 0);
      label.textContent = `${k} of ${N} units done${prefix ? "" : " (custom picks)"} · ${fmtH(remH)} h left`;
      cnt.textContent = `${k}/${N}`;
      list.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(cb => {
        cb.checked = set.has(Number(cb.dataset.u));
        cb.closest(".ucheck").classList.toggle("on", cb.checked);
      });
    };

    MODS.forEach(m => {
      const us = s.units.filter(u => unitMod(id, u.u) === m);
      if (!us.length) return;
      const head = document.createElement("div");
      head.className = "mod-head";
      head.innerHTML = `<span>Module ${MODULE_LABEL[m]}</span><button type="button">Tick all</button>`;
      $("button", head).onclick = () => {
        const set = doneSet(d, id);
        const all = us.every(u => set.has(u.u));
        us.forEach(u => all ? set.delete(u.u) : set.add(u.u));
        d.done[id] = [...set].sort((a, b) => a - b);
        paint();
        paintNeed();
        onChange();
      };
      list.appendChild(head);

      us.forEach(u => {
        const lb = document.createElement("label");
        lb.className = "ucheck";
        lb.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" data-u="${u.u}"><span class="tx">${esc(cleanUnit(u.text))}</span><span class="h">${fmtH(unitHrs(id, u.u))} h</span>`;
        $("input", lb).onchange = (e) => {
          const set = doneSet(d, id);
          e.target.checked ? set.add(u.u) : set.delete(u.u);
          d.done[id] = [...set].sort((a, b) => a - b);
          paint();
          paintNeed();
          onChange();
        };
        list.appendChild(lb);
      });
    });

    range.oninput = () => {
      const k = Number(range.value);
      d.done[id] = Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => i + 1);
      paint();
      paintNeed();
      onChange();
    };

    paint();
    psubs.appendChild(box);
  });

  paintNeed();
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/shared-editors.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/views/wizard.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   4-STEP SETUP WIZARD CONTROLLER
   ========================================================================= */

import { ICON } from "../data/icons.js";
import { $, clone, toast } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { cfg, ui, defaultCfg, setCfg, setProg, save, replan } from "../state.js";
import { renderProfile, renderRules, renderSchedule, renderProgress, normalizeDraft, editDates } from "./shared-editors.js";
import { switchTab, renderAll } from "../main.js";

export const WZ_STEPS = [
  { title: "Who's studying?", lead: "Pick your department and your SEC subject. Exam dates are already built in.", render: renderProfile },
  { title: "Session rules", lead: "Choose your preferred pace. All topics and requested revision will be included.", render: renderRules },
  { title: "Your daily schedule", lead: "Slide to choose how many sessions you’ll do each day. Set it to 0 for a day off.", render: renderSchedule },
  { title: "Where are you now?", lead: "Slide each subject to the units you've already finished. Each stop is one unit; notches mark the modules.", render: renderProgress }
];

export function openWizard(fromSettings) {
  ui.wizardFromSettings = !!fromSettings;
  ui.draft = fromSettings ? clone(cfg) : defaultCfg();
  if (!fromSettings) ui.draft = { ...defaultCfg(), dept: cfg.dept };
  if (fromSettings) { ui.draft.done = clone(cfg.done); }
  normalizeDraft(ui.draft);
  ui.step = 0;
  ui.wizard = true;
  document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
  paintWizard();
}

export function closeWizard() {
  ui.wizard = false;
  $("#wizard").classList.remove("open");
  document.body.style.overflow = "";
}

export function paintWizard() {
  const w = $("#wizard"), step = WZ_STEPS[ui.step], d = ui.draft;
  w.classList.add("open");
  const last = ui.step === WZ_STEPS.length - 1;
  w.innerHTML = `<div class="wz">
    <div class="wz-top">
      <div class="brand" style="color:var(--navy-800)"><h1>Setup</h1></div>
      ${cfg.ready ? `<button class="btn sm" id="wz-cancel">Cancel</button>` : ""}
    </div>
    <div class="wz-steps">${WZ_STEPS.map((_, i) => `<i class="${i < ui.step ? "on" : i === ui.step ? "cur" : ""}"></i>`).join("")}</div>
    <h2>${step.title}</h2>
    <p class="lead">${step.lead}</p>
    <div id="wz-body"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="wz-foot"><div>
    <button class="btn" id="wz-back" ${ui.step === 0 ? "disabled" : ""}>${ICON.left} Back</button>
    <button class="btn primary" id="wz-next">${last ? "Build my plan " + ICON.check : "Next " + ICON.right}</button>
  </div></div>`;

  const body = $("#wz-body", w);
  step.render(body, d, () => { updateWizardNext(); });

  $("#wz-back").onclick = () => {
    ui.step--;
    paintWizard();
    w.scrollTop = 0;
  };

  $("#wz-next").onclick = () => {
    if (ui.step === 2 && !editDates(d).some(x => d.days[x] > 0)) {
      toast("Pick at least one study session");
      return;
    }
    if (ui.step === 0 && d.sec === "SEC_OTH" && !d.secName.trim()) {
      toast("Give your SEC a name");
      return;
    }
    if (last) {
      finishSetup(d);
      return;
    }
    ui.step++;
    paintWizard();
    w.scrollTop = 0;
  };

  const c = $("#wz-cancel");
  if (c) c.onclick = closeWizard;
}

export function updateWizardNext() {
  /* hook for future live validation */
}

export function finishSetup(d) {
  normalizeDraft(d);
  const fresh = !cfg.ready;
  setCfg({ ...clone(d), ready: true });
  if (fresh || ui.wizardFromSettings) setProg({ sessions: {} });
  save();
  replan();
  closeWizard();
  ui.viewDate = null;
  ui.dirty = false;
  switchTab("plan");
  renderAll();
  toast("Plan built. Good luck!");
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/wizard.js ---
