/* =========================================================================
   PLAN VIEW (Roadmap Calendar Grid)
   ========================================================================= */

import { DEPTS } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { ICON } from "../data/icons.js";
import { $, esc, emptyHtml } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { focusISO, todayISO, parse, iso, niceDate, fmtH, dayNum, wdShort, monName } from "../utils/dates.js";
import { exportPng } from "../utils/export-image.js";
import { examsOf, subj, colorStyle } from "../engine/syllabus.js";
import { planDates } from "../engine/planner.js";
import { cfg, prog, plan, ui } from "../state.js";
import { switchTab } from "../main.js";

export function sessionsOn(d) {
  return plan.sessions.filter(s => s.date === d);
}

export function defaultViewDate() {
  const f = focusISO();
  return plan.dates.find(d => d >= f) || plan.dates[plan.dates.length - 1] || f;
}

export function renderPlan() {
  const root = $("#view-plan");
  if (!cfg.ready) {
    root.innerHTML = emptyHtml();
    return;
  }
  const f = focusISO();
  const exams = {};
  examsOf(cfg).forEach(s => (exams[s.examDate] = exams[s.examDate] || []).push(s));
  const days = [...new Set([...planDates(cfg), ...Object.keys(exams).filter(d => d >= cfg.start)])].sort();
  const totalS = plan.sessions.length;
  const revN = plan.sessions.filter(s => s.type === "rev").length;

  let html = `<div class="plan-toolbar"><p class="muted">Tap a day to open its study card.</p></div>`;
  html += `<div id="plan-export">
    <div class="pe-head">
      <div>
        <div class="pe-kicker">Your study roadmap · ${parse(cfg.start).getFullYear()}</div>
        <h2>${esc(DEPTS[cfg.dept].label)}</h2>
        <p>${niceDate(days[0] || cfg.start)} — ${niceDate(days[days.length - 1] || cfg.start)} <span> / </span> Day by day</p>
      </div>
      <div class="pe-chips">
        <span><b>${totalS}</b> sessions</span>
        <span><b>${fmtH(plan.sessions.reduce((a, s) => a + s.hours, 0))}</b> hours</span>
        <span><b>${revN}</b> revision</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pe-body">
      <div class="plan-key"><span>THE DAILY SCHEDULE</span><span><b class="rev-tag">REV</b> Revision session</span></div>`;

  let weekKey = null;
  days.forEach(d => {
    const dt = parse(d), mon = new Date(dt);
    mon.setDate(dt.getDate() - ((dt.getDay() + 6) % 7));
    const wk = iso(mon);
    if (wk !== weekKey) {
      if (weekKey) html += `</div>`;
      weekKey = wk;
      html += `<div class="week-label">Week of ${niceDate(wk)}</div><div class="day-grid">`;
    }
    const ss = sessionsOn(d), ex = exams[d] || [];
    const cls = ["dcell", d === f ? "today" : "", d < f ? "past" : "", ex.length ? "examonly" : ""].join(" ");
    html += `<button class="${cls}" data-d="${d}">
      <div class="top">
        <span class="num">${dayNum(d)}</span>
        <span class="wd">${wdShort(d)} · ${monName(d)}</span>
        ${d === f ? `<span class="flag no-export">${todayISO() === f ? "TODAY" : "NEXT"}</span>` : ""}
      </div>
      ${ss.map(s => `<div class="pill ${prog.sessions[s.id] ? "done" : ""}" style="${colorStyle(s.subjectId)}">
        <span class="nm">${esc(subj(s.subjectId, cfg).shortName)}</span>
        ${s.type === "rev" ? `<span class="rev-tag">REV</span>` : ""}
        <span class="hr">${fmtH(s.hours)}h</span>
      </div>`).join("")}
      ${!ss.length && !ex.length ? `<div class="rest-label">Rest & recharge</div>` : ""}
      ${ex.map(s => `<div class="exam-badge">${ICON.exam}<span>${esc(s.shortName)} exam</span></div>`).join("")}
    </button>`;
  });

  if (weekKey) html += `</div>`;
  html += `</div><div class="pe-foot">STUDY PLAN &middot; ${esc(DEPTS[cfg.dept].label.toUpperCase())}</div></div>`;
  html += `<div class="plan-bottom"><button class="btn primary" id="dl-plan">${ICON.dl} Download plan</button></div>`;

  if (!days.length) {
    html = emptyHtml("No study days left", "All exam dates have passed or no sessions are scheduled. Re-run the setup from Settings.");
  }

  const keepY = window.scrollY;
  root.innerHTML = html;
  window.scrollTo({ top: keepY, behavior: "instant" });

  root.querySelectorAll(".dcell").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    ui.viewDate = b.dataset.d;
    switchTab("day");
  });

  const dl = $("#dl-plan");
  if (dl) dl.onclick = () => exportPng($("#plan-export"), "Study-Plan.png", 1120);
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/plan-view.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/views/day-view.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   DAY VIEW (Daily Study Card & Topic Lists)
   ========================================================================= */

import { DEPTS, SUBJECTS } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { ICON, SUBJECT_SVGS } from "../data/icons.js";
import { $, esc, emptyHtml } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { focusISO, wdShort, wdName, dayNum, monName, parse, daysBetween, fmtH, niceDate, hrs } from "../utils/dates.js";
import { exportPng } from "../utils/export-image.js";
import { examsOf, subj, cleanUnit, colorStyle } from "../engine/syllabus.js";
import { cfg, prog, plan, ui, save } from "../state.js";
import { sessionsOn, defaultViewDate } from "./plan-view.js";
import { renderAll } from "../main.js";

export function renderDay() {
  const root = $("#view-day");
  if (!cfg.ready) {
    root.innerHTML = emptyHtml();
    return;
  }
  if (!plan.dates.length) {
    root.innerHTML = emptyHtml("Nothing scheduled", "There are no sessions in your plan.");
    return;
  }
  if (!ui.viewDate || !plan.dates.includes(ui.viewDate)) {
    ui.viewDate = defaultViewDate();
  }
  const d = ui.viewDate, f = focusISO();
  const idx = plan.dates.indexOf(d);

  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="day-nav">
      <button class="iconbtn" id="d-prev" aria-label="Previous day" ${idx <= 0 ? "disabled" : ""}>${ICON.left}</button>
      <div class="chip-scroll" id="chips">${plan.dates.map(x => {
        const ss = sessionsOn(x);
        return `<button class="dchip ${x === d ? "active" : ""} ${x < f ? "past" : ""}" data-d="${x}">
          <div class="w">${wdShort(x)}</div>
          <div class="n">${dayNum(x)}</div>
          <div class="d">${ss.map(s => `<i style="background:${SUBJECTS[s.subjectId].theme.border}"></i>`).join("")}</div>
        </button>`;
      }).join("")}</div>
      <button class="iconbtn" id="d-next" aria-label="Next day" ${idx >= plan.dates.length - 1 ? "disabled" : ""}>${ICON.right}</button>
    </div>
    <div class="stage"><div id="card-slot"><div id="day-card-wrap">${dayCardHtml(d)}</div></div></div>
    <div class="day-actions">
      <button class="btn primary" id="d-dl">${ICON.dl} Download as image</button>
      <button class="btn" id="d-all">${ICON.check} Mark day ${sessionsOn(d).every(s => prog.sessions[s.id]) ? "not done" : "done"}</button>
    </div>`;

  root.querySelectorAll(".dchip").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    ui.viewDate = b.dataset.d;
    renderDay();
  });

  $("#d-prev").onclick = () => { ui.viewDate = plan.dates[idx - 1]; renderDay(); };
  $("#d-next").onclick = () => { ui.viewDate = plan.dates[idx + 1]; renderDay(); };
  $("#d-dl").onclick = () => exportPng($("#day-card"), `Study-Card-${d}.png`);

  $("#d-all").onclick = () => {
    const ss = sessionsOn(d), all = ss.every(s => prog.sessions[s.id]);
    ss.forEach(s => all ? delete prog.sessions[s.id] : (prog.sessions[s.id] = true));
    save();
    renderAll();
  };

  root.querySelectorAll(".ds-check").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    const sid = b.dataset.sid;
    prog.sessions[sid] ? delete prog.sessions[sid] : (prog.sessions[sid] = true);
    save();
    renderAll();
  });

  const act = $(".dchip.active"), chips = $("#chips");
  if (act) chips.scrollLeft = act.offsetLeft - chips.offsetLeft - (chips.clientWidth - act.offsetWidth) / 2;
  fitTopicLists();
}

// Limit only the mobile preview; export clones always display the full list.
export function fitTopicLists() {
  const mobile = window.matchMedia("(max-width:600px)").matches;
  document.querySelectorAll("#day-card:not(.exporting) .ds-body ul").forEach(list => {
    const items = list.children;
    const scrollable = mobile && items.length > 3;
    if (scrollable) {
      const height = items[2].getBoundingClientRect().bottom - items[0].getBoundingClientRect().top;
      list.style.setProperty("--topic-height", Math.ceil(height) + "px");
      list.tabIndex = 0;
      list.setAttribute("aria-label", "Topics — scroll for more");
    } else {
      list.style.removeProperty("--topic-height");
      list.removeAttribute("tabindex");
      list.removeAttribute("aria-label");
    }
    list.closest(".ds-body").classList.toggle("has-more-topics", scrollable);
  });
}

export function dayCardHtml(d) {
  const ss = sessionsOn(d);
  const idx = plan.dates.indexOf(d) + 1;
  const hoursDay = ss.reduce((a, s) => a + s.hours, 0);
  const doneN = ss.filter(s => prog.sessions[s.id]).length;
  const pct = ss.length ? Math.round(doneN / ss.length * 100) : 0;
  const exToday = examsOf(cfg).filter(s => s.examDate === d);
  const upcoming = examsOf(cfg).filter(s => s.examDate >= d);
  const next = upcoming[0];
  const toNext = next ? daysBetween(d, next.examDate) : null;
  const R = 31, C = 2 * Math.PI * R;
  const n = ss.length;
  const cols = n <= 1 ? 1 : n === 2 ? 2 : n === 3 ? 3 : n === 4 ? 2 : 3;
  const W = cols === 1 ? 680 : cols === 2 ? 1080 : 1320;

  const sessionHtml = ss.map((s, i) => {
    const sub = subj(s.subjectId, cfg);
    const done = !!prog.sessions[s.id];
    const bullets = s.type === "rev"
      ? s.bullets.map(b => `<li><span class="dot"></span><span class="tx">${esc(b)}</span></li>`).join("")
      : s.units.map(u => `<li><span class="dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="tx">${esc(cleanUnit(u.text))}</span></li>`).join("");
    const cov = s.type === "rev"
      ? `<div class="cov-row"><span>Full-syllabus revision</span><strong>REV</strong></div><div class="cov-bar"><div class="after" style="width:100%"></div></div>`
      : `<div class="cov-row"><span>Course coverage<small>After this session</small></span><strong>${s.after}%</strong></div>
         <div class="cov-bar"><div class="after" style="width:${s.after}%"></div></div>`;

    return `<section class="ds ${done ? "done" : ""}" style="${colorStyle(s.subjectId)}">
      <div class="ds-head">
        <div class="ds-ic">${SUBJECT_SVGS[sub.iconId]}</div>
        <div class="ds-t">
          <h3>${esc(sub.name)}${s.type === "rev" ? `<span class="rev-tag">Revision${s.revNo ? " " + s.revNo : ""}</span>` : ""}</h3>
          <div class="session-hours">${fmtH(s.hours)} ${s.hours === 1 ? "hour" : "hours"}</div>
          <p>Session ${i + 1} · exam ${niceDate(sub.examDate)}</p>
        </div>
        <span class="ds-stamp no-export">DONE</span>
        <button class="ds-check no-export ${done ? "on" : ""}" data-sid="${s.id}" aria-label="Toggle done">${ICON.check}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ds-body"><ul>${bullets}</ul></div>
      <div class="ds-cov">${cov}</div>
    </section>`;
  }).join("");

  const foot = upcoming.length
    ? `<div class="dc-foot"><h4>${ICON.exam}Exams ahead</h4><div class="ex-list">${upcoming.slice(0, 6).map(s => {
        const n = daysBetween(d, s.examDate);
        return `<div class="ex-row"><i style="background:${s.theme.border}"></i><span class="ex-name">${esc(s.name)}</span><span class="dd">${niceDate(s.examDate)} · ${n === 0 ? "today" : n + "d"}</span></div>`;
      }).join("")}</div></div>`
    : "";

  return `<div class="dcard ${cols > 1 ? "land" : ""}" id="day-card" data-export-width="${W}" style="--card-width:${W}px;--cols:${cols}">
    <div class="dc-head">
      <svg class="notebook-sprig" aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 100 110" fill="none"><path d="M50 97C53 67 61 40 78 14" stroke="#287453" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M58 65C34 63 23 48 24 31C47 34 60 45 58 65Z" fill="#40956A" stroke="#246748" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M64 48C86 48 96 34 93 20C74 23 63 33 64 48Z" fill="#68AC75" stroke="#246748" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M73 25C58 22 53 12 57 3C72 7 77 15 73 25Z" fill="#40956A" stroke="#246748" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M51 87C30 84 19 71 22 58C40 61 51 72 51 87Z" fill="#68AC75" stroke="#246748" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M20 16L15 8M12 25L3 22" stroke="#F7B628" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>
      <div class="dc-date"><div class="mon">${monName(d).toUpperCase()}</div><div class="num">${dayNum(d)}</div></div>
      <div class="dc-meta">
        <div class="wd">${wdName(d)}</div>
        <div class="sub">${monName(d)} ${parse(d).getFullYear()} · Study day ${idx} of ${plan.dates.length}</div>
        <div class="dc-chips">
          <span class="dc-chip">${ss.length} session${ss.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}</span>
          <span class="dc-chip">${hrs(hoursDay)}</span>
          ${next && toNext > 0 ? `<span class="dc-chip gold">${esc(next.shortName)} exam in ${toNext}d</span>` : ""}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="ring no-export">
        <svg width="74" height="74" viewBox="0 0 74 74">
          <circle cx="37" cy="37" r="${R}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.2)" stroke-width="7"/>
          <circle cx="37" cy="37" r="${R}" fill="none" stroke="#FBBF24" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="${C}" stroke-dashoffset="${C * (1 - pct / 100)}"/>
        </svg>
        <b>${pct}%</b>
      </div>
    </div>
    ${exToday.length ? `<div class="exam-banner">${ICON.exam}Exam today: ${exToday.map(s => esc(s.name)).join(" · ")}</div>` : ""}
    <div class="dc-sessions">${sessionHtml}</div>
    ${foot}
    <div class="dc-brand">STUDY PLAN · ${esc(DEPTS[cfg.dept].label.toUpperCase())}</div>
  </div>`;
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/day-view.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/views/schedule-view.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   SCHEDULE TAB (24-Hr Calendar Grid, Drag/Resize/Swipe & Context Menu)
   ========================================================================= */

import { SUBJECTS } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { ICON } from "../data/icons.js";
import { $, esc, emptyHtml, toast } from "../utils/dom.js";
import {
  todayISO, now, wdName, niceDate, parse, addDays, hm,
  SCH_PX_H, SCH_PX_MIN, SCH_GUT, SCH_SNAP, SCH_EDGE,
  toMin, hhmm, snapMin, clampMin, durMin
} from "../utils/dates.js";
import { cfg, ui, save } from "../state.js";
import { openSessionDialog } from "./session-modal.js";
import { ssFill } from "./session-detail-view.js";

export function customOn(d) {
  return (cfg.custom || [])
    .filter(x => x.date === d && x.start && x.end)
    .sort((a, b) => toMin(a.start) - toMin(b.start));
}

export function layoutBlocks(list) {
  // Overlapping sessions sit side by side in columns, as in Google Calendar
  const cols = [], out = [];
  list.forEach(x => {
    let c = cols.findIndex(end => toMin(x.start) >= end);
    if (c < 0) { c = cols.length; cols.push(0); }
    cols[c] = toMin(x.end);
    out.push({ x, c });
  });
  const n = Math.max(1, cols.length);
  return out.map(o => ({ ...o, n }));
}

export function blockStyle(top, height, c, n) {
  const left = `calc(${SCH_GUT}px + (100% - ${SCH_GUT + 8}px) * ${c} / ${n})`;
  const width = `calc((100% - ${SCH_GUT + 8}px) / ${n} - 4px)`;
  return `top:${top}px;height:${height}px;left:${left};width:${width}`;
}

export function renderScheduleTab() {
  const root = $("#view-schedule");
  if (!cfg.ready) {
    root.innerHTML = emptyHtml();
    return;
  }
  if (ui.schedDetail) {
    const det = (cfg.custom || []).find(v => v.id === ui.schedDetail);
    if (det) {
      root.innerHTML = `
        <div class="ss-toolbar">
          <button class="btn" id="s-back">${ICON.left} Back</button>
          <div class="ss-toolbar-r">
            <button class="btn" id="s-edit">Edit</button>
            <button class="btn danger" id="s-del">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div id="ss-slot"></div>`;
      document.getElementById("ss-slot").appendChild(document.getElementById("ss-template").content.cloneNode(true));
      $("#s-back").onclick = () => { ui.schedDetail = null; renderScheduleTab(); };
      $("#s-edit").onclick = () => openSessionDialog(det.date, det);
      $("#s-del").onclick = () => deleteSession(det, det.date);
      ssFill(det);
      return;
    }
    ui.schedDetail = null;
  }

  if (!ui.schedDate) ui.schedDate = todayISO();
  const d = ui.schedDate, isToday = d === todayISO(), ss = customOn(d);
  const totalMin = ss.reduce((t, x) => t + durMin(x), 0);
  const hours = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, h) =>
    `<div class="sch-hr" style="top:${h * SCH_PX_H}px"><span>${String(h).padStart(2, "0")}:00</span></div>`
  ).join("");

  const blocks = layoutBlocks(ss).map(({ x, c, n }) => {
    const top = toMin(x.start) * SCH_PX_MIN, height = Math.max(30, durMin(x) * SCH_PX_MIN - 2);
    return `<div class="sch-blk ${x.done ? "done" : ""} ${ui.schedSel === x.id ? "sel" : ""}" data-id="${x.id}" style="${blockStyle(top, height, c, n)};--bg2:${SUBJECTS[x.subjectId].theme.bannerBg};--bd:${SUBJECTS[x.subjectId].theme.border}">
      <span class="bt">${esc(x.name)}</span>
      <span class="bm">${x.start}–${x.end} · ${x.units.length} unit${x.units.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}</span>
      ${x.done ? `<span class="sch-stamp">DONE</span>` : ""}
      ${ui.schedSel === x.id ? `<span class="sch-grip top"></span><span class="sch-grip bot"></span>` : ""}
      <button class="sch-edit-btn" data-id="${x.id}" aria-label="Edit session">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 17.25V21h3.75L17.81 9.94l-3.75-3.75L3 17.25zM20.71 7.04a1 1 0 0 0 0-1.41l-2.34-2.34a1 1 0 0 0-1.41 0l-1.83 1.83 3.75 3.75 1.83-1.83z"/></svg>
      </button>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");

  const nowMin = now().getHours() * 60 + now().getMinutes();
  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="sch-top">
      <span class="sch-title">Schedule</span>
      <button class="btn ${ui.schedEdit ? "primary" : ""}" id="s-edit">${ui.schedEdit ? "Done" : "Edit"}</button>
    </div>
    <div class="day-nav">
      <button class="iconbtn" id="s-prev" aria-label="Previous day">${ICON.left}</button>
      <button class="sch-date ${isToday ? "live" : ""}" id="s-pick" aria-label="Pick date">
        <span class="k">${isToday ? "Today" : wdName(d)}</span>
        <span class="v">${niceDate(d)} ${parse(d).getFullYear()}</span>
      </button>
      <button class="iconbtn" id="s-next" aria-label="Next day">${ICON.right}</button>
    </div>
    <div class="sch-stats">
      <span><b>${ss.length}</b> session${ss.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}</span>
      <span><b>${hm(totalMin / 60)}</b> planned</span>
    </div>
    <div class="sch-grid" id="s-grid" style="height:${24 * SCH_PX_H}px">
      ${hours}${blocks}${isToday ? `<div class="sch-now" style="top:${nowMin * SCH_PX_MIN}px"></div>` : ""}
    </div>
    <p class="sch-foot">Drag an empty slot to add a session. Drag a session to move it, or drag its top or bottom edge to resize. Swipe left or right-click to delete. Tap Edit, then a session, to change it. Times snap to 15 minutes.</p>`;

  $("#s-edit").onclick = () => {
    ui.schedEdit = !ui.schedEdit;
    renderScheduleTab();
  };

  root.querySelectorAll(".sch-edit-btn").forEach(b => b.onclick = (ev) => {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const x = cfg.custom.find(v => v.id === b.dataset.id);
    if (x) openSessionDialog(d, x);
  });

  $("#s-prev").onclick = () => { ui.schedDate = addDays(d, -1); renderScheduleTab(); };
  $("#s-next").onclick = () => { ui.schedDate = addDays(d, 1); renderScheduleTab(); };

  $("#s-pick").onclick = () => {
    const inp = document.createElement("input");
    inp.type = "date";
    inp.value = d;
    inp.style.cssText = "position:fixed;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;left:50%;top:50%";
    document.body.appendChild(inp);
    inp.onchange = () => {
      if (inp.value) ui.schedDate = inp.value;
      inp.remove();
      renderScheduleTab();
    };
    inp.onblur = () => setTimeout(() => inp.remove(), 200);
    (inp.showPicker ? inp.showPicker() : inp.click());
  };

  const grid = $("#s-grid");
  if (ui.schedFocus != null) {
    const m = ui.schedFocus;
    ui.schedFocus = null;
    requestAnimationFrame(() =>
      window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, grid.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY + m * SCH_PX_MIN - 160), behavior: "instant" })
    );
  }

  grid.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
    grid.dataset.ptr = e.pointerType;
    onGridPointer(e, d);
  });

  grid.addEventListener("contextmenu", (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest(".sch-blk");
    if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if (grid.dataset.ptr !== "mouse") return;
    showBlockMenu(e.clientX, e.clientY, b.dataset.id, d);
  });

  if (isToday && ui.schedSnap) {
    ui.schedSnap = false;
    const y = grid.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY + nowMin * SCH_PX_MIN;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, y - 160), behavior: "instant" }));
  }
}

export function onGridPointer(e, d) {
  if (e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
  const grid = e.currentTarget;
  const yToMin = (cy) => clampMin(Math.round((cy - grid.getBoundingClientRect().top) / SCH_PX_MIN));
  if (e.target.closest(".sch-edit-btn")) return;
  const blk = e.target.closest(".sch-blk");
  e.preventDefault();

  // ---- Create a new session by dragging on empty space
  if (!blk) {
    const anchor = snapMin(yToMin(e.clientY));
    const ghost = document.createElement("div");
    ghost.className = "sch-ghost";
    grid.appendChild(ghost);
    let a = anchor, b = anchor;
    const paintGhost = () => {
      const lo = Math.min(a, b), hi = Math.max(a, b, lo + SCH_SNAP);
      ghost.style.top = `${lo * SCH_PX_MIN}px`;
      ghost.style.height = `${(hi - lo) * SCH_PX_MIN}px`;
      ghost.textContent = `${hhmm(lo)}–${hhmm(hi)}`;
    };
    paintGhost();
    const move = (ev) => { b = snapMin(yToMin(ev.clientY)); paintGhost(); };
    const up = () => {
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
      ghost.remove();
      const lo = Math.min(a, b), hi = Math.max(a, b);
      if (hi === lo) {
        if (ui.schedSel) { ui.schedSel = null; renderScheduleTab(); }
        return;
      }
      openSessionDialog(d, null, hhmm(lo), hhmm(hi));
    };
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
    return;
  }

  // ---- Move, resize, or swipe an existing session block
  const x = cfg.custom.find(v => v.id === blk.dataset.id);
  if (!x) return;
  const rect = blk.getBoundingClientRect();
  const grip = e.target.closest(".sch-grip");
  const edge = grip
    ? (grip.classList.contains("top") ? "top" : "bottom")
    : (e.clientY - rect.top < SCH_EDGE ? "top" : (rect.bottom - e.clientY < SCH_EDGE ? "bottom" : "move"));

  const s0 = toMin(x.start), e0 = toMin(x.end), y0 = yToMin(e.clientY), startX = e.clientX, startY = e.clientY;
  let s1 = s0, e1 = e0, moved = false, swipe = false, swipeDir = 0;
  blk.classList.add("dragging");

  let pressTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    pressTimer = null;
    if (moved || swipe) return;
    ui.schedSel = x.id;
    grid.querySelectorAll(".sch-blk.sel").forEach(b => {
      if (b !== blk) {
        b.classList.remove("sel");
        b.querySelectorAll(".sch-grip").forEach(g => g.remove());
      }
    });
    blk.classList.add("sel");
    if (!blk.querySelector(".sch-grip")) {
      blk.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", '<span class="sch-grip top"></span><span class="sch-grip bot"></span>');
    }
    if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(12);
  }, 400);

  const paintBlk = () => {
    blk.style.top = `${s1 * SCH_PX_MIN}px`;
    blk.style.height = `${Math.max(30, (e1 - s1) * SCH_PX_MIN - 2)}px`;
    blk.querySelector(".bm").textContent = `${hhmm(s1)}–${hhmm(e1)}`;
  };

  let bg = null;
  const done = () => {
    window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
    window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
    blk.classList.remove("dragging");
    if (pressTimer) { clearTimeout(pressTimer); pressTimer = null; }
    if (bg) { bg.remove(); bg = null; }
  };

  const move = (ev) => {
    const dx = ev.clientX - startX, dy = ev.clientY - startY;
    if (pressTimer && (Math.abs(dx) > 6 || Math.abs(dy) > 6)) {
      clearTimeout(pressTimer);
      pressTimer = null;
    }
    if (!moved && !swipe && Math.abs(dx) > 10 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) * 1.5) {
      swipe = true;
      swipeDir = dx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    }
    if (swipe) {
      if (!bg) {
        bg = document.createElement("div");
        bg.className = swipeDir < 0 ? "sch-swipe-bg" : "sch-swipe-bg done";
        bg.style.cssText = `top:${blk.style.top};height:${blk.style.height};left:${blk.style.left};width:${blk.style.width}`;
        bg.innerHTML = swipeDir < 0
          ? `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 19c0 1.1.9 2 2 2h8c1.1 0 2-.9 2-2V7H6v12zM19 4h-3.5l-1-1h-5l-1 1H5v2h14V4z"/></svg>`
          : `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M9 16.17L4.83 12l-1.42 1.41L9 19 21 7l-1.41-1.41z"/></svg>`;
        grid.appendChild(bg);
      }
      const shift = swipeDir < 0 ? Math.max(-140, dx) : Math.min(140, dx);
      blk.style.transform = `translateX(${shift}px)`;
      bg.style.opacity = Math.min(1, Math.abs(shift) / 60);
      return;
    }

    const mdy = yToMin(ev.clientY) - y0;
    if (mdy !== 0) moved = true;
    if (edge === "move") {
      const len = e0 - s0;
      s1 = clampMin(snapMin(s0 + mdy));
      if (s1 + len > 1440) s1 = 1440 - len;
      e1 = s1 + len;
    } else if (edge === "top") {
      s1 = Math.min(e0 - SCH_SNAP, Math.max(0, snapMin(s0 + mdy)));
    } else {
      e1 = Math.max(s0 + SCH_SNAP, Math.min(1440, snapMin(e0 + mdy)));
    }
    paintBlk();
  };

  const up = (ev) => {
    done();
    if (swipe) {
      blk.style.transform = "";
      const sdx = ev.clientX - startX;
      if (swipeDir < 0 && sdx < -60) {
        deleteSession(x, d);
      } else if (swipeDir > 0 && sdx > 60) {
        cfg.custom = cfg.custom.map(v => v.id === x.id ? { ...v, done: !v.done } : v);
        save();
        renderScheduleTab();
        toast(x.done ? "Marked as not done" : "Marked as done");
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!moved) {
      if (ui.schedEdit) { openSessionDialog(d, x); return; }
      if (ui.schedSel !== x.id) { ui.schedDetail = x.id; renderScheduleTab(); }
      return;
    }
    if (s1 === s0 && e1 === e0) return;
    cfg.custom = cfg.custom.map(v => v.id === x.id ? { ...v, start: hhmm(s1), end: hhmm(e1) } : v);
    save();
    renderScheduleTab();
    toast("Session moved");
  };

  window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
  window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
}

export function deleteSession(x, d) {
  if (!confirm("Delete this session?")) {
    renderScheduleTab();
    return;
  }
  cfg.custom = cfg.custom.filter(v => v.id !== x.id);
  save();
  renderScheduleTab();
  toast("Session deleted");
}

export function showBlockMenu(x0, y0, id, d) {
  document.querySelectorAll("#sch-menu").forEach(m => m.remove());
  const m = document.createElement("div");
  m.id = "sch-menu";
  m.className = "sch-menu";
  m.style.left = Math.min(x0, window.innerWidth - 170) + "px";
  m.style.top = Math.min(y0, window.innerHeight - 100) + "px";
  m.innerHTML = `<button data-a="edit">Edit</button><button data-a="del" class="danger">Delete</button>`;
  document.body.appendChild(m);

  const x = cfg.custom.find(v => v.id === id);
  const close = () => {
    m.remove();
    document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", outside, true);
  };
  const outside = (ev) => { if (!m.contains(ev.target)) close(); };

  m.querySelector('[data-a="edit"]').onclick = () => { close(); if (x) openSessionDialog(d, x); };
  m.querySelector('[data-a="del"]').onclick = () => { close(); if (x) deleteSession(x, d); };
  setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener("pointerdown", outside, true), 0);
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/schedule-view.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/views/session-modal.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   NEW / EDIT CUSTOM SESSION BOTTOM SHEET DIALOG
   ========================================================================= */

import { SUBJECTS, MOD_ROMAN } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { ICON, SUBJECT_SVGS } from "../data/icons.js";
import { esc, clone, toast } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { niceDate, toMin, hhmm, hm } from "../utils/dates.js";
import {
  scheduleIds, subj, cleanUnit, unitMod, doneSet,
  MODE_NAMES, MODE_SLIDER, modulesOf, modUnits, coursePct,
  normUnits, usedMods, sessionName, unitCost, usedHrs, availHrs, fitsHrs
} from "../engine/syllabus.js";
import { cfg, ui, save } from "../state.js";
import { renderScheduleTab } from "./schedule-view.js";

export function noteText(draft) {
  const dur = toMin(draft.end) - toMin(draft.start),
        avail = availHrs(draft),
        used = usedHrs(draft);
  if (dur <= 0) return "End must be after start.";
  if (!fitsHrs(used, avail)) {
    return `Planned ${hm(used)} is more than the ${hm(avail)} you chose. Shorten a unit or make the session longer.`;
  }
  return `${sessionName(cfg, draft.subjectId, usedMods(draft))} · planned ${hm(used)} of ${hm(avail)}`;
}

export function saveSession(draft, existing, close) {
  if (!draft.units.length || toMin(draft.end) <= toMin(draft.start) || !fitsHrs(usedHrs(draft), availHrs(draft))) return;
  const mods = usedMods(draft);
  const rec = {
    id: existing ? existing.id : "c" + Date.now().toString(36),
    subjectId: draft.subjectId,
    moduleNo: mods[0],
    modules: mods,
    name: sessionName(cfg, draft.subjectId, mods),
    units: [...draft.units].sort((a, b) => a.u - b.u),
    date: draft.date,
    start: draft.start,
    end: draft.end,
    done: !!draft.done
  };
  if (existing) {
    cfg.custom = cfg.custom.map(v => v.id === existing.id ? rec : v);
  } else {
    cfg.custom = [...(cfg.custom || []), rec];
  }
  ui.schedDate = rec.date;
  if (!existing) ui.schedFocus = toMin(rec.start);
  close();
  save();
  renderScheduleTab();
  toast(existing ? "Session updated" : "Session added");
}

export function openSessionDialog(d, existing, startHint, endHint) {
  const ids = scheduleIds(cfg);
  const start0 = startHint || "09:00";
  const draft = existing
    ? { ...clone(existing), units: normUnits(existing.units), moduleNo: existing.moduleNo || modulesOf(existing.subjectId)[0] }
    : {
        id: null,
        subjectId: ids[0],
        moduleNo: modulesOf(ids[0])[0],
        units: [],
        date: d,
        start: start0,
        end: endHint || hhmm(Math.min(1439, toMin(start0) + 60)),
        done: false
      };

  const back = document.createElement("div");
  back.className = "sch-back";
  document.body.appendChild(back);
  const close = () => back.remove();

  let step = existing ? 1 : 0; // 0 = pick a subject, 1 = modules and units of that subject
  const addLog = {};           // unit -> times ticked in this editing session (drives ADDED tag)
  const openSet = new Set(existing ? [existing.subjectId] : []);

  const paint = () => {
    const keep = [...back.querySelectorAll(".sch-sheet, .sch-units")].map(el => el.scrollTop);
    const mods = modulesOf(draft.subjectId);
    const courseIds = ids.slice().sort((x, y) => SUBJECTS[x].examDate.localeCompare(SUBJECTS[y].examDate));
    if (!mods.includes(draft.moduleNo)) draft.moduleNo = mods[0];
    const list = modUnits(draft.subjectId, draft.moduleNo), done = doneSet(cfg, draft.subjectId);
    const dur = toMin(draft.end) - toMin(draft.start);
    const avail = availHrs(draft), used = usedHrs(draft);
    const valid = draft.units.length > 0 && dur > 0 && fitsHrs(used, avail);

    const unitsHtml = list.map(u => {
      const sel = draft.units.find(v => v.u === u.u);
      const blocked = !sel && !fitsHrs(used + unitCost(draft.subjectId, u.u, 3), avail); // 3 = Review (cheapest)
      return `<div class="unit-row ${blocked ? "off" : ""}">
        <label class="ucheck">
          <input type="checkbox" data-u="${u.u}" ${sel ? "checked" : ""} ${blocked ? "disabled" : ""}>
          <span>${esc(cleanUnit(u.text))}${done.has(u.u) ? " <small>(done)</small>" : ""}${addLog[u.u] ? ` <span class="added-tag">${addLog[u.u] > 1 ? "ADDEDx" + addLog[u.u] : "ADDED"}</span>` : ""}</span>
        </label>
        ${sel ? `<div class="sch-mode">
          <div class="slider-wrap sch-meter">
            <input class="stops" type="range" min="0" max="3" step="1" value="${MODE_SLIDER.indexOf(sel.mode)}" data-r="${u.u}" aria-label="Depth" style="--p:${MODE_SLIDER.indexOf(sel.mode) * 100 / 3}%">
            <div class="ticks"><i style="left:0%"></i><i style="left:33.33%"></i><i class="mod" style="left:66.67%"></i><i style="left:100%"></i></div>
          </div>
          <b>${hm(unitCost(draft.subjectId, u.u, sel.mode))}</b>
        </div>` : ""}
      </div>`;
    }).join("");

    const pct = avail > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round(100 * used / avail)) : 0;
    const over = !fitsHrs(used, avail);

    const pick = (id) => `<button type="button" class="sch-pick" data-pick="${id}">
      <span class="sch-ico">${SUBJECT_SVGS[SUBJECTS[id].iconId] || ""}</span>
      <span class="sch-pick-text"><b>${esc(subj(id, cfg).name)}</b><small>Exam ${niceDate(SUBJECTS[id].examDate)} · ${coursePct(cfg, id)}% done</small></span>
      <span class="sch-go" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
    </button>`;

    const saveBtn = `<button class="btn primary sch-save-ic" id="as-save" aria-label="Save session" title="Save" ${valid ? "" : "disabled"}>
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M17 3H5c-1.11 0-2 .9-2 2v14c0 1.1.89 2 2 2h14c1.1 0 2-.9 2-2V7l-4-4zm-5 16c-1.66 0-3-1.34-3-3s1.34-3 3-3 3 1.34 3 3-1.34 3-3 3zm3-10H5V5h10v4z"/></svg>
    </button>`;

    const actions = `<div class="sch-act">
      <div class="grp">${existing ? `<button class="btn danger" id="as-del">Delete</button>
        <button class="btn" id="as-done">${draft.done ? "Reopen" : "Mark done"}</button>` : ""}</div>
      <div class="grp"><button class="btn" id="as-cancel">Cancel</button></div>
    </div>`;

    back.innerHTML = step === 0
      ? `<div class="sch-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Session">
          <div class="sch-head-row"><h3>${existing ? "Edit session" : "New session"}</h3></div>
          <label class="sch-fld">Choose a subject</label>
          <div class="sch-picks">${courseIds.map(pick).join("")}</div>
          <div class="sch-act"><div class="grp"></div><div class="grp"><button class="btn" id="as-cancel">Cancel</button></div></div>
        </div>`
      : `<div class="sch-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Session">
          <div class="sch-head-row"><h3>${esc(subj(draft.subjectId, cfg).name)}</h3>${saveBtn}</div>
          <div class="sch-back-row"><button type="button" class="btn sm" id="as-back">${ICON.left} Subjects</button></div>
          <div class="sch-bar-wrap">
            <div class="sch-bar-top"><span>Time allocated</span><b class="${over ? "err" : ""}">${hm(used)} of ${hm(avail)}</b></div>
            <div class="sch-bar"><div class="sch-bar-fill ${over ? "over" : ""}" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div>
          </div>
          <label class="sch-fld">Module</label>
          <div class="sch-mod-slider">
            <input class="sch-mod-range" type="range" min="0" max="${Math.max(0, mods.length - 1)}" step="1" value="${Math.max(0, mods.indexOf(draft.moduleNo))}" data-mslider aria-label="Module">
          </div>
          <div class="sch-mod-labels">${mods.map(m => `<span class="${m === draft.moduleNo ? "on" : ""}">${MOD_ROMAN[m] || "Module " + m}</span>`).join("")}</div>
          <p class="sch-mod-info">${MOD_ROMAN[draft.moduleNo] || "Module " + draft.moduleNo} · ${draft.units.filter(v => unitMod(draft.subjectId, v.u) === draft.moduleNo).length} picked · ${coursePct(cfg, draft.subjectId, draft.moduleNo)}% done</p>
          <label class="sch-fld">Units</label>
          <div class="sch-units">${unitsHtml}</div>
          <p class="sch-note">${niceDate(draft.date)} · ${draft.start}–${draft.end}</p>
          <p class="sch-note sch-plan ${dur > 0 && fitsHrs(used, avail) ? "" : "err"}">${noteText(draft)}</p>
          ${actions}
        </div>`;

    [...back.querySelectorAll(".sch-sheet, .sch-units")].forEach((el, i) => {
      if (keep[i] != null) el.scrollTop = keep[i];
    });

    back.querySelectorAll("details.sch-course").forEach(el => el.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
      if (el.open) openSet.add(el.dataset.c);
      else openSet.delete(el.dataset.c);
    }));

    back.onclick = (e) => { if (e.target === back) close(); };
    back.querySelector("#as-cancel").onclick = close;

    back.querySelectorAll("[data-pick]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      draft.subjectId = b.dataset.pick;
      draft.moduleNo = modulesOf(draft.subjectId)[0];
      draft.units = [];
      step = 1;
      paint();
    });

    back.querySelectorAll("[data-mslider]").forEach(r => r.oninput = () => {
      const m = mods[Number(r.value)];
      if (m && m !== draft.moduleNo) {
        draft.moduleNo = m;
        paint();
      }
    });

    const refreshTime = () => {
      const avail = availHrs(draft), used = usedHrs(draft), dur = toMin(draft.end) - toMin(draft.start);
      const fits = fitsHrs(used, avail), pct = avail > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round(100 * used / avail)) : 0;
      const top = back.querySelector(".sch-bar-top b");
      if (top) {
        top.textContent = `${hm(used)} of ${hm(avail)}`;
        top.classList.toggle("err", !fits);
      }
      const fill = back.querySelector(".sch-bar-fill");
      if (fill) {
        fill.style.width = pct + "%";
        fill.classList.toggle("over", !fits);
      }
      const planEl = back.querySelector(".sch-plan");
      if (planEl) {
        planEl.textContent = noteText(draft);
        planEl.classList.toggle("err", !(dur > 0 && fits));
      }
      const saveEl = back.querySelector("#as-save");
      if (saveEl) saveEl.disabled = !(draft.units.length > 0 && dur > 0 && fits);
      back.querySelectorAll(".unit-row").forEach(row => {
        const box = row.querySelector("input[data-u]");
        if (!box || box.checked) return;
        const off = !fitsHrs(used + unitCost(draft.subjectId, Number(box.dataset.u), 3), avail);
        box.disabled = off;
        row.classList.toggle("off", off);
      });
    };

    const backBtn = back.querySelector("#as-back");
    if (backBtn) backBtn.onclick = () => { step = 0; paint(); };

    back.querySelectorAll("[data-mm]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      if (b.dataset.c !== draft.subjectId) draft.units = [];
      draft.subjectId = b.dataset.c;
      draft.moduleNo = b.dataset.mm;
      paint();
    });

    back.querySelectorAll("[data-u]").forEach(c => c.onchange = () => {
      const u = Number(c.dataset.u);
      if (!c.checked) {
        draft.units = draft.units.filter(v => v.u !== u);
        paint();
        return;
      }
      addLog[u] = (addLog[u] || 0) + 1;
      const wasDone = doneSet(cfg, draft.subjectId).has(u);
      const order = wasDone ? [0, 1, 3] : [1, 0, 3];
      const mode = order.find(m => fitsHrs(usedHrs(draft) + unitCost(draft.subjectId, u, m), availHrs(draft)));
      if (mode === undefined) {
        c.checked = false;
        toast("Not enough time in this session for this unit");
        return;
      }
      draft.units = [...draft.units, { u, mode }];
      paint();
    });

    back.querySelectorAll("[data-r]").forEach(r => r.oninput = () => {
      const u = Number(r.dataset.r), v = draft.units.find(x => x.u === u);
      if (!v) return;
      const next = MODE_SLIDER[Number(r.value)],
            before = unitCost(draft.subjectId, u, v.mode),
            after = unitCost(draft.subjectId, u, next);
      if (!fitsHrs(usedHrs(draft) - before + after, availHrs(draft))) {
        r.value = MODE_SLIDER.indexOf(v.mode);
        toast("Not enough time in this session for " + MODE_NAMES[next]);
        return;
      }
      v.mode = next;
      r.style.setProperty("--p", Number(r.value) * 100 / 3 + "%");
      r.closest(".sch-mode").querySelector("b").textContent = hm(after);
      refreshTime();
    });

    const saveBtnEl = back.querySelector("#as-save");
    if (saveBtnEl) saveBtnEl.onclick = () => saveSession(draft, existing, close);

    if (existing) {
      back.querySelector("#as-del").onclick = () => {
        if (!confirm("Delete this session?")) return;
        cfg.custom = cfg.custom.filter(v => v.id !== existing.id);
        save();
        close();
        renderScheduleTab();
        toast("Session deleted");
      };
      back.querySelector("#as-done").onclick = () => {
        const x = cfg.custom.find(v => v.id === existing.id);
        if (x) x.done = !x.done;
        save();
        close();
        renderScheduleTab();
      };
    }
  };

  paint();
  if (existing) {
    const sheet = back.querySelector(".sch-sheet");
    const row = (back.querySelector(".unit-row input:checked") || back.querySelector(".unit-row input"))?.closest(".unit-row");
    if (sheet && row) sheet.scrollTop += row.getBoundingClientRect().top - sheet.getBoundingClientRect().top - 60;
  }
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/session-modal.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/views/session-detail-view.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   SESSION DETAIL VIEW (Units, Stage Slider, Resources, PYQ & Start Modal)
   ========================================================================= */

import { SUBJECTS, MOD_ROMAN } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { SYS_BASE, SYSTEMS } from "../data/systems.js";
import { RES_RAW, RESOURCES } from "../data/resources.js";
import { OPT_PYQ } from "../data/pyq-optics.js";
import { esc, toast } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { wdName, niceDate, hm } from "../utils/dates.js";
import {
  subj, cleanUnit, unitMod, normUnits,
  MODE_NAMES, MODE_SLIDER, unitCost, usedHrs, availHrs, fitsHrs
} from "../engine/syllabus.js";
import { cfg, save } from "../state.js";
import { renderScheduleTab } from "./schedule-view.js";

export const SS = { det: null, onStage: null, onDone: null };
export const unitsData = [];

export const stageConfig = [
  { label: 'Revision', border: 'bg-[#9d5ce5]', dot: 'bg-[#9d5ce5] glow-purple', pill: 'bg-[#f3e8ff] border-[#c4b5fd] text-[#5b21b6]', ring: 'ring-[#9d5ce5]' },
  { label: 'Read', border: 'bg-[#f59e0b]', dot: 'bg-[#f59e0b] glow-amber', pill: 'bg-[#fef3c7] border-[#fcd34d] text-[#92400e]', ring: 'ring-[#f59e0b]' },
  { label: 'Deep Dive', border: 'bg-[#e11d48]', dot: 'bg-[#e11d48] glow-rose', pill: 'bg-[#ffe4e8] border-[#fda4af] text-[#9f1239]', ring: 'ring-[#e11d48]' },
  { label: 'Review', border: 'bg-[#0d9488]', dot: 'bg-[#0d9488]', pill: 'bg-[#ccfbf1] border-[#5eead4] text-[#115e59]', ring: 'ring-[#0d9488]' }
];

const RES_ICON = `<svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8z"/><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M14 2v6h6M8 13h8M8 17h5"/></svg>`;
const RES_OPEN = `<svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M4 16v2a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2v-2M7 10l5 5 5-5M12 15V3"/></svg>`;
const resHref = (p) => RES_RAW + encodeURI(p);

export function renderUnits() {
  const container = document.getElementById('units-list');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = unitsData.map((u, idx) => {
    const sc = stageConfig[u.stage];
    return `
      <div class="bg-white rounded-[20px] border border-slate-200/85 shadow-[0_4px_18px_-4px_rgba(15,23,42,0.04)] flex items-stretch overflow-hidden transition">
        <div class="w-1.5 ${sc.border} shrink-0"></div>
        <div class="flex-1 min-w-0 p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div class="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-2">
            <h4 class="font-display font-bold text-[16px] text-[#0f172a] mb-3 truncate">${esc(u.name)}</h4>
            <div class="relative flex items-center justify-between max-w-[230px]">
              <div class="absolute left-3 right-3 top-[7px] h-[2px] bg-slate-200 z-0"></div>
              ${MODE_SLIDER.map((sIdx) => {
                const stepLabel = MODE_NAMES[sIdx];
                const isActive = u.stage === sIdx;
                const activeDotClass = isActive ? stageConfig[sIdx].dot : 'bg-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-400';
                const labelClass = isActive ? 'font-bold text-[#0f172a]' : 'font-medium text-slate-500';
                return `
                  <button onclick="setUnitStage(${idx}, ${sIdx})" class="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 group focus:outline-none" title="Set ${esc(u.name)} to ${stepLabel}">
                    <span class="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full transition-all ${activeDotClass}"></span>
                    <span class="text-[11px] ${labelClass} transition-colors">${stepLabel}</span>
                  </button>`;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="w-[1px] self-stretch bg-slate-100 my-1"></div>
          <button onclick="toggleUnitEstimate(${idx})" title="Time estimate" class="flex items-center gap-2.5 pl-1 pr-1 py-1 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-50 transition text-left shrink-0">
            <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-[#f1f5f9] text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6.5 4h11M6.5 20h11M8 4v3.5a4 4 0 001.4 3L12 12l2.6-1.5a4 4 0 001.4-3V4M8 20v-3.5a4 4 0 011.4-3L12 12l2.6 1.5a4 4 0 011.4 3V20"/></svg>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div class="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-500 leading-tight">Time estimate</div>
              <div class="font-display font-bold text-sm text-slate-800 mt-0.5">${esc(u.estimate)}</div>
            </div>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

export function setUnitStage(unitIdx, newStage) {
  if (SS.onStage && !SS.onStage(unitIdx, newStage)) return;
  unitsData[unitIdx].stage = newStage;
  renderUnits();
}

export function toggleUnitEstimate() {
  // The estimate is computed from the stage; nothing to toggle.
}

export function ssSwitchTab(tab) {
  ['session', 'resources', 'pyq'].forEach(t => {
    const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + t);
    const btn = document.getElementById('tab-btn-' + t);
    const on = t === tab;
    panel.classList.toggle('hidden', !on);
    btn.className = on
      ? 'py-2.5 px-3 rounded-[12px] font-display font-bold text-[13.5px] transition-all bg-[#1849a9] text-white shadow-sm relative'
      : 'py-2.5 px-3 rounded-[12px] font-display font-semibold text-[13.5px] text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 transition-all relative';
  });
  const divider = document.getElementById('tab-divider');
  if (divider) divider.style.display = tab === 'session' ? 'block' : 'none';
}

export function sysFor(det) {
  const S = SYSTEMS[det.subjectId] || null;
  const units = normUnits(det.units).map(v => v.u).filter(u => SUBJECTS[det.subjectId].units.some(x => x.u === u));
  const inside = S ? units.filter(u => S.modules.includes(String(unitMod(det.subjectId, u)))) : [];
  return { S, units, inside, outside: units.filter(u => !inside.includes(u)) };
}

export function sysUrl(S, det, units) {
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ from: "study-plan", subject: det.subjectId, session: det.id, units: units.join(",") });
  const at = S.at && S.at[units[0]];
  return SYS_BASE + S.url + "?" + q.toString() + (at ? "#" + at : "");
}

export function openStartModal() {
  const det = SS.det;
  if (!det) return;
  const r = sysFor(det);
  if (!r.units.length) { toast("No units in this session"); return; }
  if (r.S && !r.outside.length) {
    window.open(sysUrl(r.S, det, r.inside), "_blank", "noopener");
    return;
  }
  const name = subj(det.subjectId, cfg).name;
  const list = (us) => us.map(u => "Unit " + u).join(", ");
  document.getElementById('start-modal-msg').textContent = !r.S
    ? `Dynamic notes are not available for ${name} yet. Use the notes for this session instead?`
    : r.inside.length
      ? `${r.S.name} covers ${list(r.inside)} only. Dynamic notes are not available for ${list(r.outside)} yet: use the notes for those.`
      : `${r.S.name} does not cover ${list(r.outside)}. Dynamic notes are not available for these units yet. Use the notes instead?`;

  const sysBtn = document.getElementById('start-sys-btn');
  sysBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !(r.S && r.inside.length));
  if (r.S && r.inside.length) {
    document.getElementById('start-sys-label').textContent = `Open ${r.S.name} for ${list(r.inside)}`;
    sysBtn.onclick = () => {
      closeStartModal();
      window.open(sysUrl(r.S, det, r.inside), "_blank", "noopener");
    };
  }
  document.getElementById('start-modal-backdrop').classList.remove('hidden');
}

export function closeStartModal() {
  document.getElementById('start-modal-backdrop').classList.add('hidden');
}

export function startWithNotes() {
  closeStartModal();
  ssSwitchTab('resources');
  const p = document.getElementById('panel-resources');
  if (p) p.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
}

export function setStatusState(state) {
  const badge = document.getElementById('hero-status-badge');
  const dot = document.getElementById('hero-status-dot');
  const text = document.getElementById('hero-status-text');
  const detailBox = document.getElementById('detail-status-box');
  const detailDot = document.getElementById('detail-status-dot');
  const detailText = document.getElementById('detail-status-text');

  if (state === 'progress') {
    badge.className = 'shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 text-amber-900 text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm transition';
    dot.className = 'w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500 animate-pulse';
    text.textContent = 'In progress';
    detailBox.className = 'w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0';
    detailDot.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-amber-500';
    detailText.textContent = 'In progress';
  } else if (state === 'done') {
    badge.className = 'shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm transition';
    dot.className = 'w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500';
    text.textContent = 'Done';
    detailBox.className = 'w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0';
    detailDot.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-emerald-500';
    detailText.textContent = 'Done';
  } else {
    badge.className = 'shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#ffe4e8] border border-rose-200/90 text-[#d92d20] text-xs font-semibold shadow-sm transition';
    dot.className = 'w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#e11d48]';
    text.textContent = 'Not done';
    detailBox.className = 'w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#ffe8ec] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0';
    detailDot.className = 'w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-[#e11d48]';
    detailText.textContent = 'Not done';
  }
}

export function cycleStatus() {
  if (SS.onDone) SS.onDone();
}

export function downloadResource(btn, title) {
  const span = btn.querySelector('span');
  span.textContent = 'Saved ✓';
  btn.classList.replace('text-[#1d4ed8]', 'text-emerald-700');
  toast(`Downloaded "${title}" PDF`);
  setTimeout(() => {
    span.textContent = 'Download';
    btn.classList.replace('text-emerald-700', 'text-[#1d4ed8]');
  }, 2000);
}

  const slot = document.getElementById('permutations-resource-slot');
  if (!slot) return;

    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
      <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
        <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8z"/>
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M14 2v6h6M8 13h8M8 17h5"/>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <div class="min-w-0">
        <div class="font-display font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 truncate">Gallian Ch. 5 — Permutation Groups</div>
        <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">Pages 94–110</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <button onclick="downloadResource(this, 'Gallian Ch. 5')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#f0f6ff] hover:bg-blue-100/80 border border-blue-200/80 text-[#1d4ed8] font-semibold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 transition">
      <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M4 16v2a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2v-2M7 10l5 5 5-5M12 15V3"/></svg>
      <span>Download</span>

}

function resRow(item, title, sub) {
  return `<div class="py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
      <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">${RES_ICON}</div>
      <div class="min-w-0">
        <div class="font-display font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 truncate">${esc(title || item.label)}</div>
        ${sub ? `<div class="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">${esc(sub)}</div>` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
    <a href="${esc(resHref(item.path))}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#f0f6ff] hover:bg-blue-100/80 border border-blue-200/80 text-[#1d4ed8] font-semibold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 transition">${RES_OPEN}<span>Open</span></a>
  </div>`;
}

export function resourcesHtml(det) {
  const R = RESOURCES[det.subjectId];
  if (!R) return `<p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">No files for this subject yet.</p>`;
  const units = normUnits(det.units).map(v => v.u).filter(u => SUBJECTS[det.subjectId].units.some(x => x.u === u));
  const mods = [...new Set(units.map(u => String(unitMod(det.subjectId, u))))];

  let cards = mods.map(m => {
    const modUnits = units.filter(u => String(unitMod(det.subjectId, u)) === m);
    const rows = modUnits.flatMap(u => {
      const unit = SUBJECTS[det.subjectId].units.find(x => x.u === u);
      return (R.modules[m] || []).filter(it => it.units.includes(u)).map(it => resRow(it, cleanUnit(unit.text), ""));
    });
    return `
      <section class="bg-white rounded-[20px] p-4 sm:p-5 border border-slate-200/85 shadow-2xs space-y-3">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          <h4 class="font-display font-bold text-[15px] text-slate-900">${esc(MOD_ROMAN[m] || "Module " + m)}</h4>
          <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 shrink-0">${modUnits.length} unit${modUnits.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="divide-y divide-slate-100">${rows.length ? rows.join("") : `<p class="py-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">No cutouts for these units yet.</p>`}</div>
      </section>`;
  }).join("");

  if (R.extra.length) {
    cards += `
      <section class="bg-white rounded-[20px] p-4 sm:p-5 border border-slate-200/85 shadow-2xs space-y-3">
        <div class="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3"><h4 class="font-display font-bold text-[15px] text-slate-900">Syllabus and full textbooks</h4></div>
        <div class="divide-y divide-slate-100">${R.extra.map(it => resRow(it, it.label, it.name)).join("")}</div>
      </section>`;
  }
  return `<div class="space-y-4">${cards}</div>`;
}

export function pyqHtml(det) {
  if (det.subjectId !== "OPT") return `<p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">No PYQ for this subject yet.</p>`;
  const sel = [...new Set(normUnits(det.units).map(v => v.u))]
    .filter(u => SUBJECTS.OPT.units.some(x => x.u === u))
    .sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (!sel.length) return `<p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">No units in this session.</p>`;

  const mods = [...new Set(sel.map(u => String(unitMod("OPT", u))))];
  const cards = mods.map(m => {
    const rows = sel.filter(u => String(unitMod("OPT", u)) === m).map(u => {
      const unit = SUBJECTS.OPT.units.find(x => x.u === u);
      const items = OPT_PYQ[u] || [];
      const body = items.length
        ? items.map(it => `<div class="py-3 px-3 border-t border-slate-100 first:border-t-0">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-1">
              <div class="min-w-0">
                <span class="font-display font-bold text-[13px] text-slate-900">${esc(it.t)}</span>
                <div class="text-xs text-slate-500">${esc(it.m)} ${it.y} · ${it.k} marks · ${esc(it.s)}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p class="text-[13px] text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">${esc(it.q)}</p>
          </div>`).join("")
        : `<p class="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">No PYQ mapped to this unit yet.</p>`;
      return `<details class="unit-dd" open><summary><span class="truncate">${esc(cleanUnit(unit.text))}</span><span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 shrink-0 ml-2">${items.length}</span></summary><div class="unit-list">${body}</div></details>`;
    }).join("");
    return `
      <section class="bg-white rounded-[20px] p-4 sm:p-5 border border-slate-200/85 shadow-2xs space-y-3">
        <div class="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3"><h4 class="font-display font-bold text-[15px] text-slate-900">${esc(MOD_ROMAN[m])}</h4></div>
        <div class="space-y-1">${rows}</div>
      </section>`;
  }).join("");
  return `<div class="space-y-4">${cards}</div>`;
}

export function ssUpdate(id, change) {
  const x = (cfg.custom || []).find(v => v.id === id);
  if (!x) return;
  change(x);
  save();
  renderScheduleTab();
}

export function ssFill(det) {
  SS.det = det;
  const s = subj(det.subjectId, cfg);
  document.getElementById('hero-title').textContent = s.name;
  document.getElementById('hero-sub').textContent = det.name;
  document.getElementById('detail-date').textContent = `${wdName(det.date)}, ${niceDate(det.date)} ${det.date.slice(0, 4)}`;
  document.getElementById('detail-time').textContent = `${det.start} – ${det.end}`;
  document.getElementById('detail-duration').textContent = `${hm(usedHrs(det))} of ${hm(availHrs(det))}`;
  const fillBtn = document.getElementById('fill-details-btn');
  if (fillBtn) fillBtn.remove();
  setStatusState(det.done ? 'done' : 'not-done');

  const sys = sysFor(det).S;
  const mainStart = document.getElementById('ss-main-start');
  if (mainStart) {
    mainStart.disabled = false;
    mainStart.title = sys ? 'Open ' + sys.name : 'Use the notes';
  }

  unitsData.length = 0;
  normUnits(det.units).forEach(v => {
    const u = SUBJECTS[det.subjectId].units.find(x => x.u === v.u);
    if (u) {
      unitsData.push({
        u: u.u,
        name: cleanUnit(u.text),
        stage: v.mode,
        estimate: hm(unitCost(det.subjectId, u.u, v.mode))
      });
    }
  });

  SS.onStage = (idx, mode) => {
    const next = unitsData.map((x, i) => ({ u: x.u, mode: i === idx ? mode : x.stage }));
    const total = next.reduce((t, v) => t + unitCost(det.subjectId, v.u, v.mode), 0);
    if (!fitsHrs(total, availHrs(det))) {
      toast("Not enough time in this session for " + MODE_NAMES[mode]);
      return false;
    }
    ssUpdate(det.id, x => { x.units = next; });
    return true;
  };

  SS.onDone = () => ssUpdate(det.id, x => { x.done = !x.done; });
  document.getElementById('panel-resources').innerHTML = resourcesHtml(det);
  document.getElementById('panel-pyq').innerHTML = pyqHtml(det);
  renderUnits();
}
--- END OF FILE js/views/session-detail-view.js ---

--- START OF FILE js/views/settings-view.js ---
/* =========================================================================
   SETTINGS VIEW, JSON DATA EXPORT/IMPORT & RESET ACTIONS
   ========================================================================= */

import { DEPTS, SEC_OPTIONS } from "../data/subjects.js";
import { ICON } from "../data/icons.js";
import { $, esc, clone, toast, emptyHtml } from "../utils/dom.js";
import { todayISO, hrs, wdShort, niceDate } from "../utils/dates.js";
import { activeIds, examsOf } from "../engine/syllabus.js";
import {
  cfg, prog, profiles, ui, defaultCfg,
  setCfg, setProg, save, loadProfile, replan
} from "../state.js";
import {
  renderProfile, renderRules, renderSchedule, renderProgress,
  normalizeDraft, editDates
} from "./shared-editors.js";
import { openWizard } from "./wizard.js";
import { renderPlan } from "./plan-view.js";
import { renderDay } from "./day-view.js";
import { renderAll, renderStats } from "../main.js";

export function exportData() {
  save();
  const data = {
    app: "study-plan-v4",
    version: 2,
    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    active: cfg.dept,
    profiles
  };
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" }));
  a.download = `study-plan-${todayISO()}.json`;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
  toast("Data exported");
}

export function importData(file) {
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = () => {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(reader.result);
      const incoming = data.profiles ?? (data.cfg ? { [data.cfg.dept || "physics"]: { cfg: data.cfg, prog: data.prog || {} } } : null);
      if (!incoming || typeof incoming !== "object") throw new Error("not a study plan file");
      const clean = {};
      Object.entries(incoming).forEach(([k, p]) => {
        if (!DEPTS[k] || !p || !p.cfg || !p.cfg.ready) throw new Error(`bad profile "${k}"`);
        clean[k] = {
          cfg: { ...defaultCfg(), ...p.cfg, dept: k },
          prog: { sessions: {}, ...(p.prog || {}) }
        };
      });
      if (!confirm(`Import ${Object.keys(clean).map(k => DEPTS[k].label).join(", ")}? Matching departments on this browser will be replaced; others are kept.`)) return;
      save();
      Object.assign(profiles, clean);
      loadProfile(data.active && clean[data.active] ? data.active : Object.keys(clean)[0]);
      ui.settingsDraft = false;
      ui.dirty = false;
      ui.viewDate = null;
      replan();
      save();
      renderAll();
      toast("Data imported");
    } catch (e) {
      toast("Import failed: " + e.message);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

export function renderSettings() {
  const root = $("#view-settings");
  if (!cfg.ready) {
    root.innerHTML = emptyHtml();
    return;
  }
  if (!ui.draft || ui.tab !== "settings" || !ui.settingsDraft) {
    ui.draft = clone(cfg);
    normalizeDraft(ui.draft);
    ui.settingsDraft = true;
    ui.dirty = false;
  }
  const d = ui.draft;
  const sections = [
    { k: "Profile", sum: () => `${DEPTS[d.dept].label} · ${SEC_OPTIONS.find(o => o.id === d.sec).label}`, fn: renderProfile },
    { k: "Session rules", sum: () => `${hrs(d.hours)} · ${d.maxPerDay}/day preferred · SEC ${d.secSessions}`, fn: renderRules },
    { k: "Daily schedule", sum: () => `${editDates(d).reduce((s, x) => s + d.days[x], 0)} sessions`, fn: renderSchedule },
    { k: "Progress so far", sum: () => `${activeIds(d).reduce((s, id) => s + (d.done[id] || []).length, 0)} units done`, fn: renderProgress }
  ];

  root.innerHTML = `<div class="set-grid">
    <div class="card">
      <h2 style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800">Settings</h2>
      <p class="muted" style="font-weight:600;margin-top:4px;font-size:13.5px">Every change applies straight away and rebuilds your plan from today. Exam dates never change.</p>
      <p id="set-status" class="muted" style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;margin-top:6px"></p>
    </div>
    ${sections.map((s, i) => `<details class="card" id="sec-${i}"><summary><span>${s.k}<small id="sum-${i}"></small></span></summary><div class="set-inner"></div></details>`).join("")}
    <details class="card">
      <summary><span>Exam timetable<small>fixed</small></span></summary>
      <div class="set-inner">
        <div class="exam-table">
          ${examsOf(d).map(s => `<div><i style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;background:${s.theme.border}"></i>${esc(s.name)}<span class="dt">${wdShort(s.examDate)} ${niceDate(s.examDate)}</span></div>`).join("")}
        </div>
      </div>
    </details>
    <div class="card">
      <h3 style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;margin-bottom:10px">Your data</h3>
      <div class="zone">
        <div>
          <button class="btn" id="d-export">Export data (JSON)</button>
          <p class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;margin-top:6px">Saves every department's plan and progress to one file.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <button class="btn" id="d-import-btn">Import data (JSON)</button>
          <p class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;margin-top:6px">Loads a file made by Export. Matching departments are replaced.</p>
          <input type="file" id="d-import" accept="application/json" hidden>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
      <h3 style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;margin-bottom:10px">Start over</h3>
      <div class="zone">
        <div>
          <button class="btn" id="s-wizard">${ICON.redo} Re-run the setup wizard</button>
          <p class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;margin-top:6px">Walk through all four setup screens again, from today.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <button class="btn" id="s-clear">Clear completed ticks</button>
          <p class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;margin-top:6px">Keeps your plan but un-checks every finished session.</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <button class="btn danger" id="s-reset">Delete plan &amp; setup</button>
          <p class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;margin-top:6px">Removes the current department's plan and progress and starts a new setup. Other departments are kept.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;

  const applySettings = () => {
    const status = $("#set-status");
    if (!editDates(d).some(x => d.days[x] > 0)) {
      status.textContent = "Pick at least one study session per day to rebuild.";
      return;
    }
    if (d.sec === "SEC_OTH" && !d.secName.trim()) {
      status.textContent = "Give your SEC a name to rebuild.";
      return;
    }
    status.textContent = "";
    normalizeDraft(d);
    setCfg({ ...clone(d), ready: true });
    save();
    replan();
    ui.dirty = false;
    ui.viewDate = null;
    renderStats();
    renderPlan();
    renderDay();
  };

  const setDirty = () => {
    ui.dirty = true;
    sections.forEach((s, i) => { $("#sum-" + i).textContent = s.sum(); });
    applySettings();
  };

  sections.forEach((s, i) => {
    $("#sum-" + i).textContent = s.sum();
    const det = $("#sec-" + i), inner = $(".set-inner", det);
    let built = false;
    const build = () => {
      if (built) return;
      built = true;
      s.fn(inner, d, () => {
        setDirty();
        if (i <= 1) {
          sections.forEach((dependent, j) => {
            const target = $("#sec-" + j);
            if (j >= 2 && target.open) dependent.fn($(".set-inner", target), d, setDirty);
          });
        }
      });
    };
    det.addEventListener("toggle", () => { if (det.open) build(); });
  });

  sections.forEach((s, i) => {
    const det = $("#sec-" + i);
    det.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
      if (det.open && i >= 2) s.fn($(".set-inner", det), d, setDirty);
    });
  });

  $("#s-wizard").onclick = () => openWizard(true);
  $("#s-clear").onclick = () => {
    if (confirm("Un-check every completed session?")) {
      setProg({ sessions: {} });
      save();
      renderAll();
      toast("Cleared");
    }
  };
  $("#s-reset").onclick = () => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete the ${DEPTS[cfg.dept].label} plan and setup? This can't be undone. Other departments are kept.`)) return;
    delete profiles[cfg.dept];
    const dept = cfg.dept;
    setCfg({ ...defaultCfg(), dept });
    setProg({ sessions: {} });
    save();
    ui.settingsDraft = false;
    replan();
    renderAll();
    openWizard(false);
  };
  $("#d-export").onclick = exportData;
  $("#d-import-btn").onclick = () => $("#d-import").click();
  $("#d-import").onchange = e => {
    if (e.target.files[0]) importData(e.target.files[0]);
    e.target.value = "";
  };
}

/* =========================================================================
   MAIN ENTRY POINT, APP SHELL ROUTING & INITIALIZATION
   ========================================================================= */

import { $, esc, toast } from "./utils/dom.js";
import { todayISO, daysBetween } from "./utils/dates.js";
import { examsOf } from "./engine/syllabus.js";
import { cfg, prog, plan, ui, load, replan } from "./state.js";
import { openWizard } from "./views/wizard.js";
import { renderPlan } from "./views/plan-view.js";
import { renderDay, fitTopicLists } from "./views/day-view.js";
import { renderScheduleTab } from "./views/schedule-view.js";
import { renderSettings } from "./views/settings-view.js";
import {
  setUnitStage,
  toggleUnitEstimate,
  ssSwitchTab,
  openStartModal,
  closeStartModal,
  startWithNotes,
  cycleStatus,
  fillSampleDetails,
  downloadResource,
  addPermutationResource
} from "./views/session-detail-view.js";

// Expose global handlers used by inline onclick="..." attributes in HTML templates
Object.assign(window, {
  openWizard,
  setUnitStage,
  toggleUnitEstimate,
  ssSwitchTab,
  openStartModal,
  closeStartModal,
  startWithNotes,
  cycleStatus,
  fillSampleDetails,
  downloadResource,
  addPermutationResource,
  showToast: toast
});

export function switchTab(tab) {
  ui.tab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(b => {
    b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.tab === tab);
    b.setAttribute("aria-selected", b.dataset.tab === tab);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".view").forEach(v => {
    v.classList.toggle("active", v.id === "view-" + tab);
  });
  if (tab === "settings") {
    ui.settingsDraft = false;
    renderSettings();
  }
  if (tab === "day") renderDay();
  if (tab === "plan") renderPlan();
  if (tab === "schedule") {
    ui.schedSnap = true;
    renderScheduleTab();
  }
  if (tab !== "schedule") {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "instant" });
  }
}

export function renderStats() {
  const el = $("#hdr-stats");
  if (!el) return;
  if (!cfg.ready) {
    el.innerHTML = "";
    return;
  }
  const done = plan.sessions.filter(s => prog.sessions[s.id]).length;
  const nextEx = examsOf(cfg).find(s => s.examDate >= todayISO());
  el.innerHTML = `<span>${done}/${plan.sessions.length} sessions</span>${
    nextEx ? `<span>${esc(nextEx.shortName)} exam in ${daysBetween(todayISO(), nextEx.examDate)}d</span>` : ""
  }`;
}

export function renderAll() {
  renderStats();
  if (ui.tab === "plan") renderPlan();
  else if (ui.tab === "day") renderDay();
  else if (ui.tab === "schedule") renderScheduleTab();
  else if (ui.tab === "settings") renderSettings();
}

// Event Listeners
document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(b => {
  b.onclick = () => switchTab(b.dataset.tab);
});

document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (ui.tab !== "day" || ui.wizard || /INPUT|TEXTAREA/.test(document.activeElement.tagName)) return;
  const i = plan.dates.indexOf(ui.viewDate);
  if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" && i > 0) {
    ui.viewDate = plan.dates[i - 1];
    renderDay();
  }
  if (e.key === "ArrowRight" && i < plan.dates.length - 1) {
    ui.viewDate = plan.dates[i + 1];
    renderDay();
  }
});

window.addEventListener("resize", fitTopicLists);
document.fonts.ready.then(fitTopicLists);

// Boot Application
load();
replan();
switchTab("plan");
renderStats();
if (!cfg.ready) openWizard(false);

// Register PWA Service Worker
if ("serviceWorker" in navigator && /^https?:/.test(location.protocol)) {
  navigator.serviceWorker.register("sw.js").catch(() => {});
}
--- END OF FILE js/main.js ---
